ivvāṟu kūṟakkēṭṭa ceviliyāṉavaḷ, piṉpu naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭu niṟkum. naṟṟāyum tantaikkum, taṉṉaiyarkkum aṟattoṭu niṟkum. piṉ, talaimakaṉ tamaroṭum pōntu, talai makaḷai varaintukoṇṭu, illaṟam nikaḻttā niṟpaṉ eṉka.
iṉi, kaḷavoḻukkam kuṟṟamuṭaiya taṉṟō eṉiṉ, itutāṉ, evvāṟṟāṉum oppumaiyuṭaiyā riruvar māṭṭut teyvattāṉ nikaḻnta tākalāṉum, kantaruvar vaḻakkattōṭu otta tākalāṉum, piṉpu ivviruvarumē nāṭaṟiya naṉ maṇañ ceytu kōṭalāṉum kuṟṟamuṭaittākātu. talaivaṉukkum talaivikkum uḷḷa talaiyaṉpu ivvoḻukkattil ciṟantu kāṇappaṭutaliṉ, itu kuṇamuṭaiyatu mākiṉṟatu. iṉpattiṟkuk kāraṇam aṉpē yākaliṉ, makkaḷuḷ talaivarāyiṉāriṭattu nikaḻum aṉpiṉai viḷakki, ataṉ kāriyamākiya iṉpattaik kāṭṭuvatu āṉṟōr karuttām. iṟaiyaṉārum mutaṟ cūttirattu, ‘aṉpi ṉaintiṇaik kaḷaveṉap paṭuvatu’ eṉṟum, iṟutic cūttirattu, ‘kaḷavu kaṟpeṉak kaṇṇiya vīṇṭai yōruḷa nikaḻaṉpi ṉuyarccimēṉa’ eṉṟum kūṟivaittut talaimakka ḷoḻukkam aṉpiṉaic cārntu nikaḻvateṉṟu kāṭṭi yaruḷiṉār. talaivaṟkum talaivikkum iṭaiyē tōṉṟum ivvaṉpē, piṉ ellā vuyirkaḷiṭattum aṉpu ceytaṟkum, ulakelāmāki, vēṟāy, uṭaṉumāyirukkum muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷiṭattu aṉpu ceytaṟkum, vittup pōṉṟuḷḷa teṉpatu uyttuṇar vārkku viḷaṅkum.
iṉi, ‘poyyum vaḻuvum’ mikkuḷḷa ikkālattiṟku ivvoḻukkam poruntiya taṉṟāmeṉiṉum, makkaḷ aṟivu varappeṟṟu, oruvarai yoruvar aṟintu, maṉam paṟṟiya piṉṉarē maṇañ ceytal takkateṉavum, ciṟumiyar marappāvaikku vatuvaiyāṟṟutal pōl iḷaṅ kuḻavikaṭku maṇam muṭittal ciṟitum takāteṉavum aṟika.
peṇṭirk kuriya kaṭamai palavaṟṟuḷḷum mutalāka niṟpatu, avarkaḷ kaṟpiṉiṉṟum vaḻuvātu tammaip pātukāttuk koḷḷutalē. ‘taṟkāttut taṟkoṇṭāṟ pēṇit takaicāṉṟa - coṟkāttuc cōrvilāḷ peṇ.’ eṉṟu vaḷḷuvaṉārum ataṉaiyē mutalāka vaittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. kaṟpeṉṉum oḻukkamē avarkaṭkuc ciṟanta aṇikalaṉāvatu. uyarkuṭip piṟanta peṇṭir tam kaṟpiṉai nilaināṭṭutaṟku uyiriṉai yiḻakka vēṇṭu
meṉiṉ, avvuyiriṉaiyum ōr turumpeṉak karuti viṭuvar. 
1 uyiriṉuñ ciṟantaṉṟu nāṇē nāṇiṉuñ
 ceyirtīr kāṭcik kaṟpucciṟan taṉṟeṉat
 tollōr kiḷavi pulliya neñcamoṭu.
eṉat toṉṟu toṭṭu varum tamiḻ nāṭṭup peṇṭiratu kaṟpiṉ tiṟattai āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār ciṟanteṭuttuk kūṟutaluṅ kāṇka. ippāṭṭiṉ kaṇ, talaiviyāṉavaḷ, iru mutukuravar koṭuppa maṇam nikaḻāviṭiṉum, ūḻāl taṉṉai yuṟṟāṉukkē tāṉ uriya ḷeṉṉum uṟutip pāṭuṭaiyaḷāy, itaṉai veḷippaṭuttā  toḻiyiṉ, tāy tantaiyarāl taṉ kaṟpiṟku ēlātaṉa karutappaṭṭut taṉakkum, kuṭikkum vaṭuvuṇṭā
meṉṟu añci tōḻiyai nōkki, ‘vilaiyurnta aṇikalaṉ keṭiṉum mīṇṭum vantu kūṭum; cālpu mutaliya kuṉṟiṉ uṇṭāya paḻiyaip pōkkip paḻaiya pukaḻai niṟuttal muṉivarkkum aritu eṉpar nūlōr’ eṉavum, ‘emakkuḷ nikaḻnta immaṉṟalaic cevilikku aṟivuṟuttalē naṉṟām’ eṉavum, ‘ataṉpiṉ tāy tantaiyar nammait talaivarkku aḷikka nērntilarāyiṉum, ivvaruttattaip poṟuttiruntu uyir nīppiṉ, maṟumaiyiṉum ikkūṭṭam namakku iyaivatākum’ eṉavum kūṟiṉāḷāka,
‘	muttiṉu maṇiyiṉum poṉṉiṉu mattuṇai
nērvaruṅ kuraiya kalaṅkeṭiṟ puṇarum;
cālpum viyappu miyalpuṅ kuṉṟiṉ
mācaṟak kaḻīi vayaṅkupukaḻ niṟuttal
ācaṟu kāṭci yaiyarkku mannilai
yeḷiya veṉṉār toṉmaruṅ kaṟiñar;
iruvēm āynta maṉṟa lituveṉa
nāmaṟi vuṟāliṟ paḻiyu muṇṭō?
āṟṟiṉ vārā rāyiṉu māṟṟa 
ēṉaiyula kattu miyaivatāl namakkeṉa’
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa toṭarkaḷ, ettuṇai uyariya karuttait tiṭpamuṟa viḷakki makiḻvuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa pārmiṉ! ‘kalaṅkeṭiṟ puṇarum’ eṉṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭāl, makkaṭku aḵtu illā toḻiyiṉum, uyirkku aṇiyāya oḻukka muṭaiyārē yāvarāṉum pukaḻappaṭum mēṉmaiyuṭaiya reṉpatum, aḵtuṭaittāyiruppiṉum oḻukka milātavar pōkkutaṟkariya paḻiyiṉaiyē eytuvareṉpatum peṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ittuṇai aṟivum uṟutiyum vāynta peṇ makkaḷaip peṟṟiruttaliṉum oru nāṭu peṟutaṟkuriya pēṟu yātuḷatu?
iṉi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ,
‘	kuṉṟukeḻu nāṭaṉem viḻaitaru peruviṟal
uḷḷat taṉmai yuḷḷiṉaṉ koṇṭu
cāṟayarn taṉṉa miṭāac coṉṟi
varunarkku varaiyā vaḷanakar poṟpa
malarat tiṟanta vāyil palaruṇap 
painniṇa moḻukiya neymmali yaṭicil
vacaiyil vāṉṟiṇaip puraiyōr kaṭumpoṭu
viruntuṇ ṭeñciya miccil peruntakai
niṉṉō ṭuṇṭalum puraiva teṉṟāṅku
aṟampuṇai yākat tēṟṟi.’
eṉa illaṟa viyalpu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu, talaiviyiṉ karuttu varaintu koṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttutalā yirukkumeṉa uṭkoṇṭa talaimakaṉ, avaḷai nōkkik kūṟiṟṟākat tōḻi kūṟiyatākum. itil, talaivaṉṟaṉ maṉaiyakam, palarum vantu uṇṇumpaṭi katavu akalattiṟantē kiṭakkum vāyilaiyuṭaiya teṉṟum, miṭāc cōṟṟai varuvārkkellām varaiyāmaliṭu taliṉ, viḻāk koṇṭāṭiṉāṟ pōlum celvattai yuṭaiya teṉṟum kūṟiyiruppatu, tamiḻ makkaḷatu aṟattiṉ mēm pāṭṭiṉai malai viḷakkākkukiṉṟatu. ‘paṭaippup pala paṭaittup palarō ṭuṇṇum, uṭaipperuñ celvar’ eṉap puṟattilum, ‘pukaḻnilaiiya moḻi vaḷara, aṟanilaiiya akaṉaṭṭiṟ, cōṟuvākkiya koḻuṅkañci, yāṟu pōlap parantoḻuki’ eṉap paṭṭiṉap pālaiyilum uṇavaḷittaliṉ ciṟappuk kūṟiyiruttaluṅ kāṇka. ivaṟṟāl, palaruṭaṉāka uṇṇutalē celvattup payaṉāmeṉpatum, itu peṟṟārē celvarāmeṉpatum peṟappaṭuva tāyiṟṟu. ‘malarat tiṟanta vāyil’ eṉṉuṅ karuttiṉaik kamparum, ‘aṭaiyā neṭuṅkatavu’ eṉṟu kūṟuvar. piṟarum piṟavāṟu kūṟuvar. ittakaiya maṉaiyiṉ kaṇ, paciya niṇamoḻukiṉa ney mikka aṭicilai nī iṭukaiyiṉālē, kuṟṟamillāta uyarkuṭiyiṟ piṟanta periyōr tamatu cuṟṟattōṭē viruntuṇṭu eñciyuḷḷa miccilai niṉṉōṭu uṇṇutal mēṉmaiyuṭaittu’ eṉṟum, ‘iv villaṟamē nammaik karaiyēṟṟuvatākum’ eṉṟum talaivaṉ talaivikkuk kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka. eṉavē, varaintukoṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttuvēm eṉṟāṉāyiṟṟu. illiliruntu ceyyappaṭum aṟam palavaṟṟuḷḷum viruntōmpal ciṟappuṭaittu. vaḷḷuvaṉārum viruntōmpalaiyē ciṟanteṭuttu mutaṟkaṭ kūṟuvārāyiṉar. piṟa aṟam puriyiṉum, viruntōmpal ilvaḻi, illaṟam illai yāyiṟṟeṉpatu tēṟṟam. ivvaṟam tāṉ otta aṉpuṭaiya kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyum kūṭiyiruntallatu ceyyalākātu. ‘tollōr ciṟappiṉ viruntetir kōṭalum iḻanta eṉṉai’ eṉṟu kaṇṇaki kūṟuvatāka iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum, ‘viruntu kaṇṭapō teṉṉuṟumō veṉa vetumpum’ eṉṟu cītai aḻuṅkuvatākak kamparum kūṟiyirukkum nuṭpam uḷaṅkoḷaṟ pālatu. ippāṭṭil, ‘painniṇamoḻukiya neymmali yaṭicil eṉṟamaiyāl’ viruntiṉark kaḷippatu ciṟanta vuṇavātal vēṇṭu meṉpatāyiṟṟu. ‘vacaiyil vāṉṟiṇaip puraiyōr’ eṉṟamaiyāl viruntiṉar takuti yaḷavē viruntōmpaliṉ payaṉaḷavā meṉpatāyiṟṟu. ‘viruntuṇ ṭeñciya miccil’ eṉṟamaiyāl, viruntiṉarkku iṭutaṟ poruṭṭē uṇavu aṭutal vēṇṭumeṉpatāyiṟṟu. ‘peruntakai’ eṉṟamaiyāl, aṅṅaṉam micciluṇpāṉē periya takutip pāṭuṭaiyāṉ eṉpatāyiṟṟu. ‘niṉṉōṭuṇṭalum’ eṉṟamaiyāl, maṉaivi muṉṉiruntu maṉa makiḻn tūṭṭa uṇṇa vēṇṭu meṉpatāyiṟṟu.
iṉaittuṇait teṉpatoṉṟillai viruntiṉ
ṟuṇaittuṇai vēḷvip payaṉ.
vittu miṭalvēṇṭuṅ kollō viruntōmpi
micciṉ micaivāṉ pulam.
eṉṉum maṟai moḻikaḷ iṅku niṉaikkaṟ pālaṉa. oruvaṉaiyum oruttiyaiyum paṭaittuk koṇṭu, avarkaḷ vāyilāka ulakattārkku iṉpattiṉ peṟṟiyai aṟivippāṉ pukka āciriyar, avviṉpam nilaipeṟutaṟku aṟam paṟṟukkōṭātalai yaṟintu, kaṟpoḻukkamum, viruntōmpalum mutaliya illaṟa muṟaimaikaḷai iṭaṉaṟintu kūṟivaittā reṉka.
iṉi, ippāṭṭiyaṟṟiya nallicaip pulavarāya kapilar, tām kūṟum ipporuḷiṉ cuvaiyai mēmpaṭuttuk kāṭṭutaṟkup pala tiṟappaṭṭa vaṉappuṭaiya ulakiyaṟ poruṭ kāṭciyil nammaik koṇṭu celkiṉṟār. ulakattil aḻaku mikkuṭaiya poruḷkaḷaik kāṇuṅkāl makkaḷatu aṟivu, avaṟṟil aḻunti niṉṟu, iṉpuṟutal kaṇkūṭām. attakaiya poruḷ
kaḷai vevvēṟiṭattut taṉittaṉiyākak kāṇpatiṉum, avai palavaṟṟaiyum tokuttu vaittuḷḷa kāṭci maṉṟattil orucēra kāṇuṅkāl, avaṟṟiṉ niṟaṅkaḷum, vaṭivukaḷum, kuṇaṅkaḷum, ceyalkaḷum, oṟṟumai, vēṟṟumaikaḷum eṇṇiṟanta vakaiyāyt tōṉṟa, avaikaḷai aṅṅaṉam paṭaittaruḷiya muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷiṉ varampilā āṟṟalai niṉaintu, viyantu  urukukiṉṟōm. aṅṅaṉam palporuḷum tokuttu vaittuḷa kāṭci maṉṟam tāṉum, ekkālattum evviṭattumuḷḷa yāvarkkum orucērap payaṉpaṭuvatiṉṟu. nallicaip pulavarāyiṉārē apporuḷkaḷait tam pāṭṭukkaḷil tokuttu vaittu, ekkālat tiyāvaruḷḷattum nilavumāṟu ceyya vallunar. iyaṅkiyaṟ poruḷum, nilaiyiyaṟ poruḷum ākiya ulakattup poruḷkaḷ ceyyuḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum poḻutu, avaṟṟiṉiyaṟkai piṟaḻāmal uḷḷavāṟu kūṟappaṭutal vēṇṭum. avvāṟu kūṟumiṭattum, orōvaḻip poruḷ pulappaṭātoḻiyiṉum, cuvai tōṉṟāviṭiṉum avaṟṟōṭu oppumaiyuṭaiyavaṟṟaik kaṇṭu cārttik kūṟutal vēṇṭum. oru poruḷai ataṉ taṉmai piṟaḻāmal kūṟak kēṭkumiṭattum, otta taṉmaiyuṭaiya vēṟu poruḷōṭu uvamittuk kūṟak kēṭkumiṭattum uṇṭākum makiḻcci peritu. ivaṟṟai muṟaiyē taṉmai naviṟciyeṉavum, uvamai eṉavum kūṟuvar aṇinūlār. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār uvama moṉṟē kūṟi, ataṉkaṭ pala aḻakum aṭaṅka vaitta māṭci aḷaviṭṭuraikkaṟ pālataṉṟu. piṟkālattār alaṅkārameṉap pala nūlkaḷ eḻuti vaittārēṉum, avaiyellām tamiḻil illāta aḻakiṉaik kūṟuvaṉavumalla; uḷḷa aḻakiṉai eñcātu eṭutturaippaṉavu malla; iyaṟkaiyiṉ vaḻāmal iyampuvaṉavu malla. ivvuṇmaiyiṉai, ‘niraṉiṟuttamaitta’ eṉṉum uvamaviyaṟ cūttira vuraiyil, ‘iṉi ivvōttiṉiṟ kūṟukiṉṟa uvamaṅkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟaiyum, collatikārat tiṉuḷḷum, ceyyuḷiyaluḷḷum collukiṉṟaṉa. cila poruḷ kaḷaiyum vāṅkik koṇṭu maṟṟavai ceyyuṭ kaṇṇē aṇiyāmeṉa ikkālattāciriyar nūl ceytāru muḷar. avai orutalaiyākac ceyyuṭku aṇiyeṉṟu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟappaṭā. eṉṉai? vallār ceyyiṉ aṇiyākiyum, allār ceyyiṉ aṇiyaṉṟākiyum varum tāṅkāṭṭiya ilakkaṇattiṟ citaiyā vaḻiyumeṉpatu’ eṉavum. ‘allatūum poruḷatikārattuṭ poruṭ pakutikaḷellām ceyyuṭku aṇiyākalāṉ avaiyellān tokuttu aṇiyeṉak kūṟātu vēṟu cilavaṟṟai varaintu aṇiyeṉak kūṟutal payamil kūṟṟā meṉpatu.’ eṉavum pērāciriyar viḷakkiya vāṟṟāṉaṟika. poruḷkaḷaik kiṭantavāṟu kūṟiyum, uvamittuk kūṟiyum, meyppāṭum, payaṉumuṇṭākac ceyyuḷiyaṟṟa vēṇṭil, pulava rāyiṉār palvakaip poruḷkaḷaiyum kaṇṭu kaṇṭu, avaṟṟiṉ taṉmaikaḷai nuṉittaṟintu uḷatti lamaittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. paltiṇai makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷaiyum, uṇarcci vēṟupāṭukaḷaiyum naṉkaṟintārātal vēṇṭum. maṉaviyaṟkait taṉmaikaḷai mika aṟintiruntal vēṇṭum. curuṅka uraikkiṉ ulakamē avarkkuk kallūriyākavum, ulakiluḷḷa epporuḷum avar kaṟkum pāṭamākavum amaintiruttal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ulaka viyalpai muṟṟa vuṇarnta pulavarkaḷiyaṟṟum nūlkaḷē, et tēyattiṉum em moḻiyiṉum yāvarāṉum pōṟṟappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. nam pulavar perumāṉākiya kapilar pul, maram mutalākavuḷḷa aṉaittuyirkaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷaiyum, ulakat tōṟṟaṅkaḷaiyum ārāyntaṟinta aṟivu mutircci  yuṭaiyōrāvar.
ippāṭṭilē ceṅkāntaṭ pū mutal malaiyerukkam pū iṟutiyākat toṇṇūṟ ṟoṉpatu vakaip pūkkaḷai oru cērak kūṟiyiruppatum, avaṟṟuḷ palavaṟṟai, ‘vaḷḷi taḻoṇ ceṅkāntaḷ, eripurai yeṟuḻam, vāṉ pūṅkuṭacam, maṇippūṅ karuviḷai, palliṇark kuravam, palliṇarkkāyā, tēṅkamaḻ pātiri, koṅkumutir naṟuvaḻai, maṇikkulaik kaṭkamaḻ neytal, tūṅkiṇark koṉṟai, cuṭarp pūntōṉṟi’ eṉa avvavaṟṟiṉ ciṟappiyalpu tōṉṟumāṟu ēṟṟa aṭaikaḷuṭaṉ kūṟiyiruppatum maram, ceṭi, koṭi vakaikaḷil ivvāciriyarkkuḷḷa paranta ārāycciyaiyum, ivaratu niṉaiviṉ māṭciyaiyum naṉku pulappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉṉum ippāṭṭilē ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷaik kūṟumiṭattu, nelliṉaik koṇṭa neṭiya mūṅkilait tiṉṉaṟku mēlnōkki niṉṟu varuntiṉa yāṉai, avvaruttan tīrumpaṭi taṉatu kaiyaik kompiṉ mēlēṟṟit toṅka viṭṭuk koṇṭiruttalum; ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir kāyum vemmai mikka poḻutil, vāṉilē paṟakkum paṟavaikaḷ tām virumpum cēkkaikaḷiṟ cellutalum; tiṉaittāḷāl vēynta kuṟuṅ kāṟ kuṭicaiyil, maṉaivi yeṭuttuk koṭuppa matuvaiyuṇṭu kaḷittup puṉaṅ kāppōṉ kāvaṟṟoḻilai maṟantiruttalum; yāṉai puṉattait tiṉṟu aḻittu viṭukaiyaik kaṇṭa kāṉavar, ciṉam mikku, villai nāṇēṟṟi ampeytum, taṭṭaiyaip puṭaittum, kāṭellām kalleṉum ōcaiyuṇṭāka vāyai maṭittu vīḷaiyeḻuppi ataṉai yōṭṭutalum; aṅṅaṉam ōṭṭappaṭṭu matac cerukkuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭa vēḻam maraṅkaḷai muṟittu, iṭiyēṟu pōl muḻaṅki, kaiyaic curuṭṭi nilattē yeṟintu kaṭuki varutalum; talaimakaṉ talaiviyatu oḷi poruntiya nutalait tuṭaittut tōḻiyiṉ mukam nōkkic cirittalum; avaṉ talaiviyai varaintu koṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttutal meyyeṉṟu terivittaṟ poruṭṭu, malai ucciyil uṟaikiṉṟa murukak kaṭavuḷai vāḻtti vaṇaṅki, vañciṉaṅ kūṟi, iṉimai mikka aruvi nīr kuṭittalum; piṟavum, iyaṟkai vaḻāmal, ōviyam varaintu kāṭṭiṉāṟpōla aḻaku peṟak kūṟiyiruppatiliruntu, puḷ, vilaṅkukaḷiṉ ṟaṉmai kaḷaiyum, makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷaiyum, maṉaviyaṟ kaiyaiyum evvaḷavu naṉṟāka ivar aṟintuḷḷāreṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟatu.
maṟṟum, itil talaivaṉ ṟaṉmai kūṟuṅkāl, ‘naṟiya mayirccantaṉam pūci, anta īram pularumpaṭi viralālalaittup  piṇippu viṭuttu, akiṟ pukaiyūṭṭukaiyiṉālē, vaṇṭukaḷ moykkumpaṭi akiliṉ ney kalakkap peṟṟu, nīlamaṇi pōlum niṟattiṉai yuṭaittāyk kaṭaikuḻaṉṟu vaḷarn tirukkum, eṇṇey pūcappeṟṟa kuṭumiyilē palvakai malar kaḷālum toṭukkappeṟṟa toṭaiyalaiyum, tāḻai maṭaliṟ kaṭṭiya kaṇṇiyaiyum murukaṉeṉṟu accamuṟumpaṭi cūṭi, aḻakiya picci malarāṟ ṟoṭutta oru kāḻākiya mālaiyaic cuṟṟi, acōkiṉatu taḷirait tiraṇṭa tōḷilē vīḻn talaikkumpaṭi oru kātilē ceruki, valiyamaintu, akaṉṟu, uyarntu, cāntu pūcappeṟṟu, uttama vilakkaṇa mākiya mūṉṟu varikaḷaiyuṭaittāy viḷaṅkum mārpilē, toṉṟu paṭṭu varum pēraṇikalaṅkaḷōṭē naṟiya mālaikaḷai yaṇintu, uṭuttavuṭai tuḷakkamaṟa nuṇṇiya toḻilkaḷaiyuṭaiya kacciṉai iṟukak kaṭṭi, taṭakkaiyil villai yeṭuttu ampu kaḷait terintu piṭittu,poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa nalla vīrakkaḻal peyarum poḻutellām tiruntiya aṭiyilē ēṟṟiḻivu ceyyumpaṭiyāka, pakaip pulattaip pāḻākkum valiyamainta vēliṉaiyuṭaiya vīraraip pōlē ciṉattāṟ cerukkuṟṟut tammēl oṉṟu neruṅkun tōṟum vekuḷum, mūṅkil muḷaipōlum paṟkaḷaiyum, periya ukiraiyu muṭaiya nāykaḷ imaiyāta kaṇkaḷai yuṭaiyavāy vaḷaittuk koṇṭu pōtara, āṉēṟu pōl vantāṉ’ eṉṟum;
mālaip poḻutiṉ ṟaṉmai kūṟuṅkāl, ñāyiṟu malaiyaic cērntu maṟaitalāl māṉkaṇam marattaṭikaḷilē tiraḷa, āṉ kūṭṭam kaṉṟukaḷai yaḻaikkum kuralaiyuṭaiyavāy maṉṟukaḷ niṟaiyap pukutalaic ceyya, vaḷainta vāyaiyuṭaiya aṉṟil paṉaiyiṉ uṇ maṭalilēyiruntu pēṭṭai yaḻaikka, aravutāṉ mēytal kāraṇamāka maṇiyiṉai yumiḻa, āyarkaḷ palaviṭaṅkaḷiṉum niṉṟu āmpaleṉṉum paṇṇiṉaiyuṭaiya kuḻaliṭattu iṉiya icaiyai eḻuppa, āmpaliṉ aḻakiya itaḻkaḷ taḷaiyaviḻa, antaṇar mālaip poḻutiṟ ceyyum kaṭaṉkaḷai nikaḻtta, vaḷamikka maṉaikaḷil aḻakiya toṭiyiṉai yuṭaiya makaḷir, viḷakkiṉaik koḷutti vaittut tām ceyyuṅ kaṭaṉkaḷaic ceyya, vaṉattiṉ kaṇ vāḻkiṉṟavarkaḷ vāṉai yaḷāviya paraṇiṉmēlē tīkkaṭai kōlāl neruppai yuṇṭākki yerippa, mukil malaiyiṭattaic cūḻntu kaṟuppa, kāṭṭiluḷḷa vilaṅkukaḷellām kalleṉṉum ōcaiyuṭai yavāy oṉṟaṟ koṉṟu māṟik kūppiṭa, paṟavaikaḷ kuṭam paiyiliruntu āravārikka mālai tuṉṉiṟṟu’ eṉṟum; avvavaṟṟiṉ ṟaṉmaiyai ārāyntu iyaṟkai neṟi vaḻuvātu kūṟiyiruppatu mika makiḻcci viḷaippatākiṉṟatu.
iṉi, ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ muppattaintu uvamaikaḷ āṇṭāṇṭuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, karuttoṟṟumai yuḷḷa aintaṉai viṭuttu, ēṉaiya muppatu uvamaikaḷai īṇṭut tarukiṉṟām.
ceviliyiṉ muṉivukkum talaiviyiṉ varuttattiṟkum añcum tōḻiyiṉ accattiṟku, ikalpuriyum iruperu vēntark kiṭai niṉṟu cantu ceyvikkum cāṉṟōratu accamum; tuyyaiyuṭaiya talai vaḷaintu muṟṟiṉa periya tiṉaikkatirukku, yāṉaiyatu kompiṉmē lēṟṟit toṅka viṭṭirukkum tutikkaiyum; kārkālattu iṭiyiṉ muḻakkattiṟku, muraciṉ atircciyum; miṉṉalukku, murukaṉ acuraraik kolla eṭutta vēṟpaṭaiyum; malaiyucciyiṉiṉṟum viḻukiṉṟa aruvikku, viḷaṅkukiṉṟa veṇṭukilum; malaip pāṟaiyiṟ cuṉaiyiṉ nīrukku, paḷiṅkaik karaittuc corintu vaitta nīrum; ciṟiya mutukilē tāḻntu kiṭakkum piṉṉiya kūntalukku, poṉṉilē yaḻuttiṉa nīlamaṇiyum; eṟuḻampūviṉ niṟattiṟku, neruppiṉ niṟamum; cenniṟap pūkkaḷaip parappi vaittaṟku, arakkiṉai virittu vaittalum; acōkiṉatu taḷiriṉ niṟattiṟku, neruppiṉ niṟamum; vēṭṭai nāyiṉ ciṉattiṟku, vēl vīrariṉ ciṉamum; nāyiṉ pallukku, mūṅkil muḷaiyum; talaiviyaik kaṇṭu virumpiyaṭaiyum talaivaṉukku, vēṟu pulattuḷḷa  putiya āvaik kaṇṭu aṇaiya varum viṭaiyum; immeṉa imirum vaṇṭiṉōcaikku, naṭṭarākam muṟṟup peṟṟa pālai yāḻai vācikka vallavaṉ taṉ kaiyālē vācitta narampiṉōcaiyum; talai makaṉ taḻai paranta pūṅkompai muṟittu vēṭṭai nāykaḷai yaṭittalukku, mata yāṉai pūṅkompai muṟittu ōcci niṟṟalum; kuṭicaiyiliruntu puṉaṅkāppōṉatu maṉaiviyiṉ nōkkiṟku, māṉ piṇaiyum; kāṉavaratu villukku, ciṉam mikka pāmpum; matayāṉaiyiṉ piḷiṟutalukku, urumēṟṟiṉ muḻakkamum; matakkaḷiṟṟiṉ varukaikku, kūṟṟuvaṉatu varukaiyum; naṭukkamuṟṟa makaḷirkku, teyvamēṟiṉa mayilum; makaḷir kaikōt tiruttaṟku, kaṭampiṉatu aṭimarattai neruṅkac cūḻnta mālaiyum; makaḷir aruviyilē kāl taḷarntu cellutaṟku, iṭi karaiyiṉiṉṟa vāḻai carintu cellutalum; malaiccāralil tēṟalai ayiṉṟa mayil taḷarvataṟku, āṭu makaḷ kaḻāyk kayiṟṟilē ēṟi āṭukiṉṟa tāḷattiṉāl āṟṟātu taḷarutalum; ceṅkāntaṭ pūkkaḷ parantu kiṭakkiṉṟa nilattiṟku, aḻakiya pala kaccup paranta nilamum; maṉaiyiṉkaṇ varuvōrkkellām cōṟiṭutaṟku, viḻāk koṉṭāṭutalum; vaḷainta vāyaiyuṭaiya aṉṟiliṉōcaikku, ūtu kompiṉōcaiyum; paṟavaikaḷiṉoliyōṭu mālaip poḻutu tuṉṉutaṟku, ciṉam mikka vēntaṉ amariṉkaṭ cellutalum; talaiviyiṉ ṟōḷukku, iḷa mūṅkilum; imai cōrntu aḻaku keṭṭuk kalaṅkum talaiviyiṉ kaṇkaḷukku, peruntuḷi yeṟintu maḻai peyyappaṭṭa malarum; iravilē talaivaṉ varum vaḻiyiṉ ētam niṉaintu kalakkamuṟum talaivikku, valaiyiṟpaṭṭa maññaiyum; uvamaiyākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ciṟappu mutaliyaṉa nilaikkaḷamāka, viṉai, payaṉ, mey, uru eṉpaṉa paṟṟit tōṉṟum ivvuvamaṅ kaḷellām, ‘uvamaiyum poruḷum ottal vēṇṭum’ eṉat tolkāppiyaṉār kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭavāṟē amaintu, pāṭṭiṉai aḻaku ceytu, iṉpam viḷaippaṉavātal kāṇka.
iṉi, veḷippaṭaiyākak kūṟum iv vuvamam pōlaṉṟi, nuṇṇuṇarvāṉ ārāyntu kāṇattakka uḷḷuṟai yuvamam eṉpatoṉṟuṇṭu. ivvuḷḷuṟai yuvamantāṉ paṇṭaic cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷil malintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. akap poruṭ ceytikaḷil veḷippaṭak kūṟutal nākarikamaṉṟeṉat tōṉṟumavaikaḷai maṟaittuk kūṟutaṟ poruṭṭu, iḵtu iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. talaivaṉ mutalāyiṉār nāṭu mutaliyavaikaḷaip puṉainturaikku miṭattu, kēṭpōr nuṇṇuṇarvāṉ ārāyntu, ap puṉainturaiyai uvamai yākak koṇṭu vēṟu poruḷ koḷḷumāṟu kūṟuvatē uḷḷuṟai yuvamam ākum. iṅṅaṉam kūṟuvatāl nākarika muṟai vaḻuvāmaiyum, iru poruḷai oru colliṟ kūṟalākiya curuṅkak kūṟutalum, kēṭpōr aṟivu nuṇuki niṉṟuṇarntu iṉpuṟutalum ākiya pala payaṉ viḷaikiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻiṉ oppuyarvaṟṟa māṭcikku iv vuḷḷuṟai vuvamam oṉṟu tāṉē cāṉṟāta lamaiyum. ippāṭṭiṉ kaṇ, talaivaṟkum talaivikkum nikaḻnta kūṭṭattaic cevilikku aṟivikkum tōḻiyāṉavaḷ, talaimakaṉ evvāṟṟāṉum tammiṉ mikkāṉ eṉpatum, avaṉuṟavu tamakku eytutaṟ kariyateṉpatum, attuṇaip perumaiyuṭaiyāṉ tamakku eḷiyaṉāy vantu taṇṇaḷi ceytatu tamatu nalviṉaip payaṉē yeṉpatum uṇarttak karutiṉāḷ. ivaṟṟai veḷippaṭak kūṟiṉ ivaḷ talaiviyai avamatittā ḷeṉṟum, ivaḷāṉēyē icceyal nikaḻntuḷḷa teṉṟum cevilikkuc ciṉamuṇṭākum. kūṟāviṭiṉ kūṭṭa muṇmai yuṇarnta cevilikkut taṉ makaḷ yārō iḻi takavuṭaiyā ṉoruvaṉukku uriya ḷāyiṉaḷēyeṉṟu kavaṟci mikum. in nilaimaiyil yātu ceyvāḷ tōḻi?  tammai yāṟṟu nīr iḻuttuc cellum poḻutu, karaiyēṟṟiṉavaṉ ittaṉmaiyāṉa nāṭṭaiyuṭaiyaṉ eṉṟu nāṭṭiṉaic ciṟappittuk kūṟuvāḷāy, talaivaṉ perumai mutalāyiṉa uyttuṇarumāṟu uḷḷuṟaiyākak kūṟiviṭṭāḷ. ‘paḻutta miḷaku cintik kiṭakkiṉṟa pāṟaiyiṭattu, nīṇṭa cuṉaiyilē, nekiḻntu utirnta māmpaḻattālum palāppaḻattālum viḷainta tēṟal iṟāliliruntu īkkaḷait taḷḷi yukutta tēṉālē vantu tiraḷa, attēṟalait taṉakku eḷiya nīrākak karuti yuṇṭa mayilāṉatu, viḻāc cey kaḷattil āṭal makaḷ kaḻāyk kaḷiṟṟil ēṟi yāṭukiṉṟa tāḷattiṉāl tāṉ āṟṟātu taḷarumāṟu pōlat taḷaruñ cāralai yuṭaiyatum, vāṉaiyaḷāviya ucciyilē kiḷaitta ceṅkāntaḷiṉ pūkkaḷ, varaiyara makaḷir  viḷaiyāṭutaliṟ ṟannalam ciṟitu keṭṭuk kīḻē vantu parantu, nalla pala kaccu viritta kaḷampōla aḻaku peṟṟa iṭattiṉai yuṭaiyatumākiya malai poruntiṉa nāṭaṉ,  eṉpatu tōḻi kūṟiyatu. itaṉkaṭ kūṟiya miḷaku, mā, palā, tēṉiṟāl, mayil, ceṅkāntaṭpū eṉṉum kuṟiñcik karup poruḷkaḷāl malaivaḷam veḷippaṭai. itaṉai uḷḷuṟai yuvamamākak koḷḷumiṭattu, ‘miḷaku ukka pāṟai annilattu mākkaḷuṟai kiṉṟa ūrākavum, neṭuñcuṉai talaivaṉ kuṭiyākavum, māmpaḻattālum palāp paḻattālum viḷainta tēṟal tantaiyālum tāyālum uḷaṉākiya talaivaṉākavum, iṟāṟṟēṉ ivaraik kūṭṭiṉa pālvarai teyvamākavum, tēṟalaiyuṇṭa mayil uyarnta talaivaṉait taṉ kulattiṟku ottāṉākak karuti nukarnta talaiviyākavum, attēṟaliṟ piṟanta kaḷippuk kaḷavoḻukkattāṟ piṟanta pēriṉpamākavum, mayil āṭavāṟṟāt taṉmai varuntik kuṟainta taṉmaiyākavum’ koḷḷappaṭum. iṉṉum, ‘uyarnta nilattē niṉṟu maṇakkiṉṟa kāntaḷ, varaiyara makaḷirāṟ kīḻ nilattē parantu, avviṭattaik kaccu virittāṟ pōla aḻaku peṟuttu meṉṟataṉāl, nammi luyarcciyaiyuṭaiya talaivaṉ, namatu nal viṉaiyāṟ ṟaṉatu perumaikaḷ tāṉu moḻintu ivviṭattē vantu kūṭi, namakkum uyarcciyuḷa tākki, nammai aḻaku peṟuttukiṉṟāṉ.’ eṉṟamai koḷḷappaṭum. uccimēṟ pulavarkoḷ nacciṉārkkiṉiyar virittapaṭi iṅku uḷḷuṟai yuvamam kāṭṭappaṭṭateṉka.
iṉi, ippāṭṭiliruntu, vēṟupaṭṭa pala uruviṉaiyuṭaiya kaṭavuḷarai malartūviyum, naṟumpukai yeṭuttum, cantaṉam mutaliyavai koṭuttum vaḻipaṭutalum, muttu, maṇi, poṉ mutaliyavaṟṟāl ciṟanta aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceytalum, aracar tēr uṭaiyarātalum, iraṇṭu vēntar amar puriyum poḻutu aṟivuṭaiyōr iṭainiṉṟu cantu ceytalum, peṇ koṭukkum muṉ kuṭiyum, kuṇamum, cuṟṟattutaviyum mutalāya vaṟṟai oppittup pārttu eṇṇittuṇiya vēṇṭutalum, murukak kaṭavuḷ muraciṉaiyum, pūṇiṉaiyum, eḵkiṉaiyum uṭaiyarātalum, aruviyiṉṟirai pōlum veṇmaiyum, meṉmaiyum uṭaiya tukilkaḷ neyyappaṭutalum, vīrarāyiṉār vil, ampu, vēl eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṟ pōr puritalum, naṭṭarākam mutaliya paṇkaḷai yāḻilē vācittalum, ūrkaḷil tiruviḻā naṭakkumiṭattu viṟaliyar kaḻāyk kūttu āṭutalum, cōṟṟu viḻāk koṇṭāṭappaṭutalum, kaṭavuḷaic cāṉṟu vaittu vañciṉaṅ kūṟutalum, cūḷuṟutaṟku aṭaiyāḷamākat taṇṇīr kuṭittalum, mālaip poḻutilē makaḷir viḷakkēṟṟi vaittu illuṟai teyvattai vaṇaṅkutalum, antaṇar canti ceytalum, kōvalar kuḻal vācittalum, cuṟṟattār peṇ tara nāṭaṟiya naṉmaṇañ ceytalum, piṟavum akkālac  ceyti kaḷāka aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
iṉṉum, ippāṭṭil, teyvap pakutiyākak kaṭavuḷar, tēvar, varaiyara makaḷir eṉpōrum; makkaṭ pakutiyāka muṉivar, antaṇar, cāṉṟōr, aracar, kāṉavar, kōvalar, ūrkāvalar, viṟaliyar eṉpōrum; vilaṅkiṉ pakutiyil yāṉai, puli, nāy, āṉ, viṭai, piṇai, māṉ, yāḷi, karaṭi, āmāṉēṟu, mutalai eṉpaṉavum; puḷḷiṉ pakutiyil kiḷi, mayil, aṉṟil, vaṇṭu, curumpu eṉpaṉavum; paṟavait tokutiyum; ōraṟiviṉavākiya maram mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ pakutiyil oruvaḻit tokuttuk kūṟap paṭṭa toṇṇūṟṟoṉpatuṭaṉ, kaṭampu, palā, paṉai, miḷaku, tiṉai eṉpaṉavum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maṭaṅkal, cūr, picācu, pāntaḷ eṉpaṉavum kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. nilam, nīr, tī, vaḷi, vāṉ eṉpaṉavum; malai, kaṭal, mukil, miṉ, iṭi eṉpaṉavum; ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, ñāyiṟṟiṉ maṇṭilam,ñāyiṟṟiṉ ēḻ pari, ñāyiṟu maṟaiyum malai eṉpaṉavum;muttu , maṇi, poṉ, pāmpiṉ maṇi, paḷiṅku, arakku eṉpaṉavum; puviyiṉ vēṟāya ulakamum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. makkaḷiṉ uṭai, aṇi, uṇavu mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ, ippāṭṭiṉāl aṟiyap paṭuvaṉa muṉ eḻutiyavaṟṟuṭ kāṇka.
kiḷiyōṭṭuṅkaruvikaḷ taḻal, taṭṭai, kuḷir mutaliyaṉa veṉṟum, mutalai, iṭaṅkar, karām eṉpaṉa iṉa vēṟupā ṭeṉṟum aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
‘iruvēm āynta maṉṟal’ eṉpataṟku, ‘talaivaṉum, yāṉum, perumaiyum, uraṉum, accamum, nāṇum nuṇukiya nilaiyāṟ piṟanta kantaruva  maṇam’ eṉṟum, ‘akaliru vāṉam’ eṉpataṟku ‘ēṉaip pūtaṅkaḷ viritaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa kariya ākāyam’ eṉṟum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyirukkum poruḷ niṉaikkattakkaṉa. eḷitiṟ poruḷpaṭum toṭarkaḷukku iṅṅaṉam orō vaḻi ariya poruḷ kūṟuvatu ivarkkiyalpu. ivar coṟkaḷai māṭṭēṟ ṟākak koṇṭu poruḷ ceytirukku miṭaṅkaḷil, kiṭantavāṟē vaittup poruḷ ceytaṟ kēṟṟavaikaḷai ārāyntaṟika.
ippāṭṭil ēṟakkuṟaiya āyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatu coṟkaḷirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ amayam, pāyam, cēmam, piracam, camam eṉpaṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷ. muracu, anti, muttu, maṇi eṉpaṉa iru moḻikkum potuc coṟkaḷ. ñamali, coṉṟi, pāṇi eṉpaṉa ticaic coṟkaḷ. ivvāṟṟāl nūṟṟukku oru col viḻukkāṭum itil vēṟṟuc col varavillai yeṉpatu uṇarattakkatu.
iṉi, ippāṭṭil muppattiraṇṭu aḷapeṭaic coṟkaḷum, āṟu ‘ceyyu’ eṉṉum vāypāṭṭuc coṟkaḷum vantuḷḷaṉa. ikkālattē cilar aḷapeṭaiyai ikaḻntu kūṟuvar. vēṟu cilar evviṭattum aḷapeṭaiyāka eḻutuvar. ippāṭṭiṉum, itupōṉṟa ēṉaic caṅkac ceyyuḷiṉum, aḷapeṭai vantuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷai aṟivuṭaiyōr nuṇukiyuṇariṉ, avvavviṭattu aḷapeṭaiyiruttaliṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmai pulappaṭum. iṅkē cāṉṟāka oṉṟu kāṭṭutum. ‘tumpai tuḻāay cuṭarppūn tōṉṟi’ eṉpatu ippāṭṭiluḷḷa ōr aṭi. itiluḷḷa aḷapeṭaiyai eṭuttuviṭiṉ, ‘tuḻāy’ eṉpatu oru cīrātalillai. itu cīrātaṟ poruṭṭu vēṟu collaic cērttiṭil maṟṟoṉṟu virittalum, ōcaiyiṉmaiyum mutaliya kuṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭātal kūṭum. ivvōr aḷapeṭaiyāl cīr cemmaiyātalum ōcaiyuṭait tātalum, mōṉai peṟutalum, muṉ piṉ aṭikaṭku ēṟṟavāṟu amaitalum ākiya pala nalaṉ viḷaintaṉa. cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷiluḷḷa iṉṉōraṉṉa nuṇukkaṅkaḷait tamiḻt tuṟaiyil iṟaṅki yaṟiyātār eṅṅaṉam kāṇavallarāvar?
ippāṭṭil, virintuḷḷa vēṟṟumai yurupu aimpatukku mēlillai. avaṟṟuḷḷum pala oppup poruḷil varum iṉṉurupākum. pāṭṭiluḷḷa coṟkaḷellām perum pālum iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṉku eḻuttukkaḷālāṉavaiyē. ivvāṟṟāl iḵtu evvaḷavu coṟ curukkamum, tiṭpamum uṭaiyateṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkum. ippāṭṭiṉ poruḷai ippoḻutu uṇarttutaṟku mūṉṟu maṭaṅkiṟku mēlāṉa eḻuttukkaḷum, coṟkaḷum vēṇṭiyuḷḷaṉa veṉka.
iṉi, ikkuṟiñcip pāṭṭiṉkaṇ piṟa tiṇaikaṭkuriya poḻutum, pū mutaliyaṉavum orōvaḻi vantiruttaṟku viti.
‘	uripporu ḷallaṉa mayaṅkavum peṟumē.
ennila maruṅkiṟ pūvum puḷḷum 
annilam poḻutoṭu vārā vāyiṉum
vanta nilattiṉ payatta vākum.’
eṉṉum akattiṇai yiyaṟ cūttiraṅkaḷākum. ‘ennila maruṅkil’ eṉṉum cūttira vuraikkaṇ, nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, ‘kapilar pāṭiya peruṅ kuṟiñciyil varaiyiṉṟip pū mayaṅkiyavāṟu kāṇka’ eṉṟār. ataṉāl, peruṅkuṟiñci yeṉavum ippāṭṭukku ōr peyaruṇṭeṉpatu peṟṟām. kuṟiñcit tiṇaip pāṭṭukkaḷil ituvē aḷavāṟ peritāyiruttaliṉ, peruṅ kuṟiñci yeṉappaṭṭatu pōlum? maṟṟum ippāṭṭiṉ arumai, perumaikaḷai aṟivuṭaiyōr ārāyntu makiḻvārāka kapilar pāṭiya ēṉaic ceyyuṭkaḷaiyum iṅṅaṉam ārāyiṉ. mika viriyumākaliṉ, iṉi avaṟṟiṉ amaitikaḷaic curuṅkavē kūṟuvāmeṉka.
pūviṉ pakuti
                                        vaḷḷita
ḻoṇceṅ kānta ḷāmpa laṉiccam
taṇkayak kuvaḷai kuṟiñci veṭci
ceṅkoṭu vēri tēmā maṇiccikai
uritunā ṟaviḻttot tuntūḻ kūviḷam
eripurai yeṟuḻañ cuḷḷi kūviram
vaṭavaṉam vākai vāṉpūṅ kuṭacam
eruvai ceruviḷai maṇippūṅ karuviḷai
payiṉi vāṉi palliṇark kuravam
pacumpiṭi vakuḷam palliṇark kāyā
virimala rāvirai vēral cūral
kurīip pūḷai kuṟunaṟuṅ kaṇṇi
kurukilai marutam viripūṅ kōṅkam
pōṅkan tilakan tēṅkamaḻ pātiri
cerunti yatiral peruntaṇ caṇpakam
karantai kuḷavi kaṭikamaḻ kalimāt
tillai pālai kallivar mullai
kullai piṭavañ ciṟumā rōṭam
vāḻai vaḷḷi nīṇaṟu neytal
tāḻai taḷavam muṭṭāṭ ṭāmarai
ñāḻal mauval naṟuntaṇ kōkuṭi
cēṭal cemmal ciṟuceṅ kurali
kōṭal kaitai koṅkumutir naṟuvaḻai
kāñci maṇikkulaik kaṭkamaḻ neytal
pāṅkar marāam palpūn taṇakkam
īṅkai yilavan tūṅkiṇark koṉṟai
aṭampama rātti neṭuṅkoṭi yavarai
pakaṉṟai palācam palpūm piṇṭi
vañci pittikam cintu vāram
tumpai tuḻāay cuṭarppūn tōṉṟi
nanti naṟavam naṟumpuṉ ṉākam
pāram pīram paiṅkuruk katti
āraṅ kāḻvai kaṭiyirum puṉṉai
narantam nākam naḷḷiru ṇāṟi
māyiruṅ kuruntum vēṅkaiyum piṟavum
arakkuvirit taṉṉa parēram puḻukuṭaṉ
uraiyiṟutiyil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa
iru veṇpākkaḷil oṉṟu
‘	niṉkuṟṟa millai niraitoṭiyum paṇpuṭaiya 
ḷeṉkuṟṟam yāṉu muṇarkalēṉ - poṉkuṟ
ṟaruvi koḻikku maṇimalai nāṭaṉ
ṟeriyuṅkāṟ ṟīya tilaṉ.’
5. kuṟiñcikkali ārāycci
kuṟiñcikkali eṉpatu caṅkattuc cāṉṟōrālē ceyyap paṭṭu akkālattē tokukkappaṭṭaṉavākiya 1eṭṭut tokaiyu ḷoṉṟākiya kalittokaiyil iraṇṭāvatu pakutiyākum. kalittokai kaṭavuḷ vāḻttuc ceyyuḷ oṉṟum, pālai muppattaintum, kuṟiñci irupattoṉpatum, marutam muppattaintum, mullai patiṉēḻum, neytal muppattu mūṉṟum āka nūṟṟaimpatu ceyyuṭkaḷai yuṭaiyatu. pālai, kuṟiñci, marutam, mullai, neytal eṉa immuṟaiyē kōkkap paṭṭuḷatu. aintiṇaikaḷaip palvakaiyāṉum muṟaippaṭuttal tolkāppiyaṉār karutteṉa uṇarka. pālaikkali mutaliya aintu pakutiyum muṟaiyē peruṅkaṭuṅkōṉ, kapilar, marutaṉiḷanākaṉār, cōḻaṉ nalluttiraṉ, nallantuvaṉār eṉpavarkaḷāṟ pāṭap paṭṭaṉaveṉṟu,
peruṅkaṭuṅkōṉ pālai kuṟiñci kapilaṉ
marutaṉiḷa nākaṉ marutam - aruñcōḻaṉ
nallut tiraṉmullai nallan tuvaṉeytal
kalvivalār kaṇṭa kali.
eṉṉum veṇpāvāṟ ṟerikiṉṟatu. ‘purivuṇṭa’ eṉṉum neytaṟ kalic ceyyuḷuraiyil, ‘colleccamum kuṟippeccamu mākat tam pēraṟivu tōṉṟa āciriyar nallantuvaṉār ceyyuṭ ceytār’ eṉṟum, kaṭavuḷ vāḻttiṉ uraiyil, ‘ātalāṉ īṇṭup pālaittiṇaiyaiyum tiṇaiyāka āciriyar nallantuvaṉār kōttā reṉṟu kūṟuka’ eṉṟum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyiruttalāl, neytaṟkaliyai iyaṟṟiṉārum, pālaikkali mutaliyavaṟṟaik kōttārum nallantuvaṉā reṉpatu avar karuttātal peṟappaṭum. ittokaiyiluḷḷa aintu pakutiyum taṉittaṉi ovvoruvarāṟ pāṭappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, aim pakutiyaiyum cērttu ōr tokai yākkiṉamaiyē tokut tāratu ceykai yātal vēṇṭum. aiṅkuṟuṉūṟum, patiṟṟup pattum tokuttāruṭaiya ceykaiyum ittuṇaiyē yākum. itiluḷḷa ceyyuṭka ḷellām kalippā ākaliṉ, ittokai pāṭṭāṟ ṟokukkap paṭṭateṉka. itu kalittokai yeṉṟum, kali yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkum.
‘	1nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉu mulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum
pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkaṅ
kaliyē paripāṭ ṭāyiru pāṅkiṉum
uriyatāku meṉmaṉār pulavar,’
eṉṟu āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟutaliṉ, akattiṇaikkuc ciṟappurimaiyuṭaiyatu kalippāvām eṉka. ittokaiyiluḷḷa pākkaḷiṉ ōcaiyiṉpamum, piṟa arumai, perumaikaḷum kaṟṟu vallōr yāvarum oruṅkē pārāṭṭun takaiyaṉa.
itiluḷḷa kuṟiñcikkali kapilar pāṭiyateṉṟu muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu. vēṟu yāvartām pāṭiyiruppar? ivvāciriyar kuṟiñciyait tamakkē urimaiyākkik koṇṭaṉareṉṉiṉ, atu puṉainturai yākātu. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷ kaliyiṉ vakaiyākiya kaliveṇpāvum, ottāḻicaik kaliyum, koccakak kaliyum mutaliyavāka uḷḷaṉa. ippākkaḷ kūṟum poruḷum pala tuṟaippaṭṭaṉa vākum. ivai aṟitaṟku ariyavātal paṟṟiyē ittokaikku uraikaṇṭa āciriyar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar iṉṉa iṉṉa uṟuppukkaḷ koṇṭu ivvāṟu muṭitaliṉ, iḵtu iṉṉa pāvāku meṉṟum, iḵtu iṉṉa tuṟaiyaiyuṭaiya teṉṟum ovvoru pāṭṭilum viḷakkic cellukiṉṟār.
itiluḷḷa irupattoṉpatu pāṭṭukkaḷilum, taṉic cīr kaḷoḻiya eḻunūṟṟaimpatu aṭikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟil nūṟṟirupattu nāṉku uvamaikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai yaṉṟi, uḷḷuṟai yuvamaṅkaḷum, uruvakaṅkaḷum palavuḷḷaṉa. maṉōtattuva iyaṟkai vaḻāmal iṉpattiṉ kāraṇamāya oḻukkattiṉaik kūṟappukunta ippāṭṭukkaḷil, avaiyēyaṉṟi, ettaṉaiyō pala nītikaḷum, iyaṟkai yuṇmaikaḷum potintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. ivaiyiṟṟai yuṇarvārkku akkālattil tamiḻ evvaḷavu uyariya nilaiyil irunta teṉpatum, tamiḻarāyiṉār evvaḷavu mēmpāṭuṟ ṟiruntaṉa reṉpatum aṅkai nelli yeṉa viḷaṅkā niṟkum.
iṉi, itiluḷḷa uvamaikaḷil iṉṟiyamaiyātavaṟṟai urainaṭaip paṭuttu iṅku eḻutukiṉṟām. pūtta vēṅkai marattaip puliyeṉṟu karutik kaṟuvukoṇṭu ataṉaṭiyiṟ kuttiya matayāṉai, taṉ kompiṉaip paṟikkalāṟṟātu kūvik koṇṭu varuntuvatu, iṟaivaṉ iṟaiviyuṭaṉ vīṟṟirunta kayilai malaiyai yeṭukkaluṟṟa arakkar kōmā ṉākiya irāvaṇaṉ, ammalaiyiṉ kīḻk kiṭantu varuntuvatu pōlum eṉavum; talaivaṉatu kūṭṭam peṟātu talaivi polivaḻin tiruppatu, nīraṟṟa nilattiṟ payirum, poruḷillāṉ iḷamaiyum, aṟaṉiṉṟā mūp peytiṉāṉ maṟumaiyum poliviḻantiruttal pōlum eṉavum; talaivaṉ kūṭṭattāl talaivi  aḻaku peṟṟiruppatu, maḻaiyiṉaip peṟṟa nilattiṟ payirum, aruḷuṭaiyā ṉiṭattu uṇṭākiya ākkamum, ceyyuṅ kūṟupāṭaṟintavaṉ celvamum aḻaku peṟṟiruttal pōlum eṉavum; tiṉaippuṉaṅ kāppār paraṇilē yeritta akiṟ pukaiyāl oḷi maḻuṅkiya matiyam malaittalaiyil porunti yiruppatu, ammalaiyil tēṉiṉ iṟāl iruppatu pōlum eṉavum; tiṉaikkatir muṟṟit tāḻntiruppatu. naṟkuṇamuṭaiya makaḷir nāṇattāl iṟaiñciyiruppatu pōlum eṉavum; malaiyiṭattil kāntaḷiṉ kottu uyarntu acaivatu, koṭicciyar murukavēḷaik kai kuvittut toḻuvatu pōlum eṉavum; talaimakaṉ mātarār nalattai nukarntu avarait tuṟappatu, vaṇṭu tātiṉaiyuṇṭu pūvait tuṟappatu pōlum eṉavum; talaimakaṉ vāymaiyil poy tōṉṟumāyiṉ, atu tiṅkaḷiṭattē nerupput tōṉṟiyatu pōlum eṉavum; avaṉatu aruḷilē koṭumai tōṉṟumāyiṉ, atu niḻaṟkuḷattu nīril niṉṟa kuvaḷai ventatu pōlum eṉavum; avaṉatu toṭarpil naṭpiṉmai tōṉṟumāyiṉ atu ñāyiṟṟiluḷḷē iruḷ tōṉṟiyatu pōlum eṉavum; talaivaṉatu aṟattaiyē virumpiṉa kōṭutalillāta neñcam, oru kūṟṟiṟ cāyāta tulākkōl pōlum eṉavum; kōṅku pūtta pakka malaikaḷ poṉṉaṇi yaṇinta yāṉaikaḷ pōlum eṉavum; kāntaḷ mukaimēṟ poruntiyirukkum tumpi, mātarār kaiyilaṇinta nīlak kaṭaic ceṟi pōlum eṉavum; mūṅkil muḷaiyiṉ kaṇṇaip potintiruntu kaḻaṉṟu viḻum pāḷai, nuṇ poṟiyai yuṭaiya māṉiṉ ceviyaip pōlum eṉavum; vāḻaik kulaiyil tūṅkum vaḷainta kāy, puliyiṉ aṭiyaip pōlum eṉavum uvamikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
etiretir ōṅkiya malaiyiṉ cāralil iḷañāyiṟṟiṉ katiraḻakaik koṇṭa māṇikkap pāṟaiyil niṟkum vēṅkai maram, taṉ aḻakiya kompukaḷiṉ mēlē iru malaiyiṉiṉṟum aruvi vīḻap peṟṟiruppatu, centāmarai malariṉ akavitaḻil vīṟṟirukkum tirumakaḷ taṉatu tirumuṭiyiṉ mēlē aḻakiya iraṇṭu yāṉaikaḷ pūvuṭaṉ kūṭiya nīraic coriyumpaṭi yiruppataṉaiyum;1 cuṉaiyiṭattu vaḷarnta kāntaṭ kulaiyāṉatu, nīruṇṇukiṉṟa maṇiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya pāmpiṉaiyum; yāṉaiyum, puliyum pōr ceyyumiṭattē, vēṅkaiyiṉ pūṅkompeṉṟu karutip puliyaic cūḻntum, vēṅkaic ciṉaiyeṉṟu karuti yāṉaiyiṉ pukar mukattai yaṇukiyum tirintu koṇṭiruk kiṉṟa vaṇṭu, ciṉamikkup pōrpuriyum iru vēntarai naṭpākkum tiṟaṉaṟintu cantu ceyvāraiyum; talaimakaḷ talaivaṉatu aḷiyai nacciyiruppatu, vāṉam pāṭippuḷ vāṉiṉ tuḷiyai nacciyiruppataiyum; talaivaṉ kūṟuvataṉaiyē talaivi kūṟuvatu, cārntār kūṟuvataṉaiyē malai etiroliyākak kūṟuvataiyum; talaiviyiṉ kaivaḷaiyukuvatu, talaivaṉatu malaic cāraliṟ kōṭaṟ pū ukuvataiyum; nīr coriyum kaṇkaḷ, maḻaiyēṟṟa nīlattaiyum; talaiviyiṉ māmainiṟam, poṉṉuraitta maṇiyiṉ niṟattaiyum; villiṉ mēluḷḷa poṟikaḷ, pāmpiṉ poṟiyaiyum; talaivaṉ talaiviyaip pārāṭṭuvatu, malaiccāralil māṉkuḻavi vaḷarppār ceyalaiyum pōlumeṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷa.
puliyāṉatu taṉ ceviyilē maṟaintu vantu pāynta viṭattu, ataṉ mārpiṉait taṉ kōṭṭiṉ nutiyāṟ kuttit tiṟantu māṟupāṭu tīrntu niṉṟa yāṉaikku, nūṟṟuvar talaivaṉākiya turiyōtaṉaṉaik kuṟaṅkiṉiṉṟum uyirpōkki niṉṟa vīmacēṉaṉaiyum; puliyaik kutti māṟupāṭu tīrntu taṉ cuṟṟattiṟku naṭuvē kūṭit tiriyum yāṉaikku, mallaratu maṟattiṉaik keṭuttaruḷiya tirumālaiyum; kāntaḷiṉ cevviya mukaikku, pakai vēḻattiṉ nutaliṉaik kutti viḷaṅkum yāṉaiyiṉ kompaiyum; talaivi kaṇ paṉi kaluḻvataṟku, kayalmīṉ nīrumiḻvataiyum; talaiviyatu nutaliṉ vēṟupaṭṭa niṟattiṟku, pīrkkam pūviṉ niṟattaiyum; talaiviyiṉ melliya viralaiyuṭaiya kaikku, malarnta naṟavam pūvaiyum; talaivaṉ talaiviyiṉ kaikaḷaiyeṭuttut taṉ kaṇkaḷ maṟaiyumpaṭi vaittu veytāka uyirppataṟku, kollaṉ ulaimūkku vevvitāka uyirppataiyum; talaivaṉ talaiviyiṉ meym muḻutum taṭavi iṉpuṟuttuvataṟku, yāṉai tāṉ virumpiya piṭiyai aruḷ ceyvataiyum; poṉ vakirkaḷai yaṇin tirukkum aimpālākiya kūntalukku, miṉṉalkaḷ iṭaiyē piḷantōṭum koṇmūvaiyum; muttukkaḷ patitta toṭi kaḷukku, vaḷarnta tāmarai vaḷaiyattaiyum; tōḻiyiṭattē taṉkuṟai kūṟutaṟkuc ceṉṟa talaivaṉ, piṉpu ataṉaik kaiviṭṭu, avaḷ mukattaip palamuṟaiyākap pārttu niṟpataṟku, aṟivuṭaiyōr celvam kuṉṟi vaṟumaiyuṟṟapoḻutu tam iṭukkaṇ kaḷaiyat takka kēḷiriṭañ ceṉṟu, tam kuṟaiyai avarkku vāyviṭṭuc collat toṭaṅkip piṉpu colla māṭṭā tiruppataṉaiyum; talaiviyiṉ mukattaic cārntu viḷaṅkum kūntalukku, matiyaip poruntiya kariya mukilaiyum; piṉṉiya mayiriṉkaṇ nuṇṇiya nūlāṟ kaṭṭiya malarkaḷukku, karum pāmpiṭattē ataṉ kariya niṟattōṭu māṟupaṭṭu viḷaṅkum kārttikai yeṉṉum mīṉaiyum uvamai kūṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
piṉ varum ceyyuṭ pakutikaḷil uḷḷa  uvamaikaḷum kāṇka:
‘	nilleṉa niṟuttā ṉiṟuttē vantu 
nutalu mukaṉun tōḷuṅ kaṇṇum 
iyaluñ collu nōkkupu niṉaii
aitēyn taṉṟu piṟaiyu maṉṟu
maitīrn taṉṟu matiyu maṉṟu
vēyamaṉ ṟaṉṟu malaiyu maṉṟu
pūvamaṉ ṟaṉṟu cuṉaiyu maṉṟu
mella viyalu mayilu maṉṟu
collat taḷaruṅ kiḷiyu maṉṟu
eṉavāṅku, 
aṉaiyaṉa palapā rāṭṭi.’	(kuṟiñci - 16)
                                   ‘akaṉmati 
tīṅkatir viṭṭatu pōla mukaṉamarn
tīṅkē varuvā ḷivaḷyārko lāṅkēyōr
vallavaṉ ṟaiiya pālaikol nallār
uṟuppelāṅ koṇṭiyaṟṟi yāḷkol 
                                                        veṟuppiṉāl
vēṇṭuruvaṅ koṇṭatōr kūṟṟaṅkol āṇṭār
kaṭitivaḷaik kāvā viṭutal.
‘	āytūvi yaṉameṉa vaṇimayiṟ peṭaiyeṉat
tūtuṇam puṟaveṉat tutaintaniṉ ṉeḻiṉalam
mātarkoṇ māṉōkkiṉ maṭanallāy niṟkaṇṭārp
pētuṟūu meṉpatai yaṟitiyō vaṟiyāyō.’
‘	nuṇaṅkamait tiraḷeṉa nuṇṇiḻai yaṇaiyeṉa
muḻaṅkunīrp puṇaiyeṉa vamaintaniṉ ṟaṭameṉṟōḷ
vaṇaṅkiṟai vāleyiṟ ṟannallāy niṟkaṇṭārk
kaṇaṅkāku meṉpatai yaṟitiyō vaṟiyāyō.’ 
(kuṟiñci - 20)
vēyeṉat tiraṇṭatōḷ veṟikamaḻ vaṇaraimpāl
māṉveṉṟa maṭanōkkiṉ mayiliyaṟ ṟaḷarpolki
āycilam pariyārppa valiroḷi yiḻaiyimaippak
koṭiyeṉa miṉṉeṉa vaṇaṅkeṉa yātoṉṟun
terikallā viṭaiyiṉkaṭ kaṇkavar poruṅkōṭa
vaḷamaicā luyarciṟappi ṉuntaitol viyaṉakar
iḷamaiyā ṉeṟipantō ṭikattantāy kēḷiṉi. 
	(kuṟiñci - 21)
vāruṟu vaṇaraimpāl vaṇaṅkiṟai neṭumeṉṟōṭ
pēreḻiṉ malaruṇkaṭ piṇaiyeḻiṉ māṉōkkiṟ 
kāretir taḷirmēṉik kaviṉpeṟu cuṭarnutaṟ
kūreyiṟṟu mukaiveṇpaṟ koṭipuraiyu nucuppiṉāy
(kuṟiñci - 21)
iṉi, itil vantuḷḷa uḷḷuṟai yuvamaṅkaḷ pala. pāṭṭiṉ mutaluṟuppākiya taravukaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa uḷḷuṟai amain
taṉavē. avaiyaṉaittaiyum, kāṭṭaluṟiṉ mika viriyumātaliṉ, cāṉṟāka oṉṟu kāṭṭutum.
tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam nikaḻnta piṉṉar, tōḻiyāṉavaḷ talaivaṉai yeyti, ‘nī talaiviyai varaintu koṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttutal vēṇṭum’ eṉpatu tōṉṟak kūṟukiṉṟavaḷ, ‘vaḷai
kiṉṟa mūṅkiliṉ kōlai nelluṭaṉē vaḷaittut tiṉkiṉṟa peṇ yāṉai, ilai potuḷiya vāḻaikaḷ ceṟinta iṭattē pukuntu, paḻattai nukarntu, varuṭaimāṉiṉ maṟi parantu tiritarum iṭattil, viṭiyaṟ kālattē, tuyil koḷḷum veṟpiṉai
yuṭaiyāṉē’ eṉṟu viḷikkiṉṟāḷ. mūṅkil nel viḷain tiruppatum, vāḻai ceṟintiruppatum, yāṉai avaṟṟai yuṇpatum, varuṭai māṉ parantu tirivatum ākiya ivaiyellām malaiyilē kāṇappaṭuvaṉa. iṅṅaṉam malaiyiyaṟkai kūṟu mukattāl, tōḻi tāṉ karutiya vēṟu poruḷait talaivaṉukku uṇarttu kiṉṟāḷ. ‘muṉ tammiṭattut tāḻntu niṉṟa ariya talaivaṉait talaivi ippoḻutu peṟṟu nukarun taṉmaiyum, avaṉ varaintu koṇṭuḻi, avaḷ avaṉatu maṉaikkaṇ nalivaṟa iruntu iṉpan tuyttu, avaṉ cuṟṟattiṭai illaṟam nikaḻttit taṅkun taṉmaiyum’ tōḻi karutiya uḷḷuṟaiyākum. ‘mūṅkilai micainta piṭi, tammiṭattut tāḻnta talaivaṉaip peṟṟu nukarnta talaiviyākavum, piṉṉar atu vāḻaic ceṟivilē pukuntu paḻattai nukarnta taṉmai, avaṉ varaintu koṇṭuḻi, avaḷ maṉaikkaṇ nalivaṟa iruntu avaṉai nukarntatākavum, puli mutaliyaṉaviṉṟi maṟi parantu tiriyumiṭattē tuyilutal, alar kūṟuvāriṉṟi avaṉatu cuṟṟattiṭai illaṟam nikaḻttit taṅkun taṉmaiyākavum uḷḷuṟai yuvamaṅ koḷka.’ iṅṅaṉam aḷaviṉṟik kiṭakkum uḷḷuṟaiyum, veḷip paṭaiyum āṉa uvamaikaḷiṉ peṟṟiyai ārāyntu kāṇuṅkāl, ak kālattup pulavarāyiṉār evvaḷavu nuṇṇaṟivu vaḷamum, cennā vaṉmaiyum paṭaitta mētakkō reṉpatum, tamiḻ moḻi ettuṇaic cevviyum, cuvaiyum, cīrmaiyum, kūrmaiyum poruntiyirunta teṉpatum yāvarkkum pulaṉām. 
iṉi, itil nāṭakattiṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāta iṉpac cuvaiyum  nakaic cuvaiyum tōṟṟuvikkum toṭarkaḷ āṇṭāṇṭuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟai uraippaṭuttuk kāṭṭutum.
kaḷaviṟ kūṭṭattiṉ piṉpu, talaivaṉ maṇam pēciyataṟkut tamar oruvāṟṟāl uṭaṉpaṭṭamaiyait tōḻi talaivikkuk kūṟit tāṉum avaḷum varaivu kaṭitiṉ muṭitaṟ poruṭṭu varaiyuṟai teyvattiṟkuk kuravaiyāṭuṅkāl pāṭiya ‘koṇṭu nilai’kaḷil, tōḻiyāṉavaḷ talaiviyai nōkki, ‘malai nāṭaṉākiya talaivaṉum nīyum kaliyāṇa kālattē paṇṭu oruvarai yoruvar kaṇṭaṟiyātīr pōla naṭappīrkaḷō! yāṉum uṅkaḷ paḻaṅ kēṇmaiyaik kaṇṭaṟiyātēṉ pōl maṟaippēṉmaṉ!’ eṉṟu kūṟiyavaḷ, piṉpu, ‘talaivaṉ maṇak kōlattaik kāṇāmal kaiyāl maṟaikkappaṭum kaṇkaḷum kaṇkaḷeṉṟu kūṟappaṭumō?’ eṉṟāḷ; atu kēṭṭa talaivi ‘ēṭi! yāṉ niṉṉuṭaiya kaṇṇālē talaivaṉ kōlattai mikavum kāṇpēṉaṉṟō?’ eṉṟāḷ. ataṟkut tōḻi, ‘appaṭi yāyiṉ, neyta litaḻpōlum niṉkaṇ tāṉē eṉkaṇ āvatāka!’ eṉṟu kūṟi makiḻntaṉaḷ; eṉṉum karuttukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa (kuṟiñci - 3).
talaiviyāṉavaḷ talaivaṉuṭaiya kāṭci viruppum, āṟṟāmaiyum, taṉatu vēṭkaiyum pulaṉāmāṟu tōḻikkuk kūṟiṟṟākavuḷḷa ōr pāṭṭiṉ poḻippu: “oṇṭoṭīi, ītoṉṟaik kēṭpāyāka; teruviṉkaṇ nām viḷaiyāṭum maṇalāṟ ceyta ciṟṟilaik kālāl aḻittum, nam kūntaliṉ mālaiyaik kaiyāl aṟuttum, viḷaiyāṭum pantiṉai yeṭuttuk koṇṭu ōṭiyum, nām nōvat takka toḻilkaḷaic ceyyum ciṟu paṭṭiyāṉavaṉ, muṉṉoru nāḷ aṉṉaiyum yāṉum oruṅkirunta vaḷavilē vantu, ‘illil uṟaivōrkaḷē! nīr vēṭkai yuṭaiyēṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ; kūṟa, aṉṉaiyum, ‘avaṉukku uṇṇu nīraip poṉ vaḷḷattāl ūṭṭi vā’ eṉṟu coṉṉamaiyiṉ, yāṉum avaṉuṭaiya paṭṭimaiyai aṟiyātu ceṉṟēṉ; ceṉṟa piṉ, avaṉ eṉṉuṭaiya vaḷaiyaṇinta muṉ kaiyaip paṟṟi, eṉṉai nalintamaiyāl, yāṉ alamantu, ‘tāyē! ivaṉoruvaṉ ceytataṉaip pārāy’ eṉṟu kūviṉēṉ; uṭaṉē tāy alaṟik koṇṭu ōṭivara, yāṉ avaṉ ceytataṉai maṟaittu, ‘uṇṇu nīr vikki varuntiṉāṉ kāṇ’ eṉṟu oru poy kūṟiṉēṉ; tāyum avaṉatu mutukaip palakāl aḻittaḻittut taṭava, av vañcakaṉṟāṉ, eṉṉait taṉ kaṭaikkaṇṇālē kolvāṉ pōṟ pārttu, nakaik kūṭṭam ceytu ceṉṟāṉ; itu kāṇ nikaḻntatu eṉṟu talaivi tōḻikkuk kūṟiṉāḷ” eṉpatu. (kuṟiñci - 15).
talaivaṉ tam vēṭkai mikutiyāl nikaḻttiyaṉa ivai yeṉṟu talaivi tōḻikkuk kūṟuvāḷāy, “tōḻi! avaṉṟāṉ, ‘poṟpūvāṟ ceyta kōtaiyum, toṭi ceṟinta muṉkaiyum, aṇaipōlum meṉṟōḷum uṭaiyāy! niṉ aṭiyilē yāṉ uṟaitaṟku nī aruḷātiruttal niṉakkup poruntiya toṉṟō?’ eṉṟu kūṟi, kariya kūntaliṉ muṭiyai iṭai viṭātu mikappiṭittu, atil nalamuṟak kaṭṭiṉa oru mālaiyai viralāl muṟaiyākac cuṟṟi, ataṉai mōntu pārttaluñ ceytāṉ; atuvēyaṉṟi, naṟavampū alarntāṟ pōlum eṉ melliya kaiyait tāṅkikkoṇṭu, aruḷ poruntiya tam civanta kaṇ maṟaiyumpaṭi vaittuk kollaṉ ulaimūkkup pōla vevvitāka uyirtaluñ ceytāṉ; atuvēyaṉṟi, toyyil eḻutiṉa mārpai iṉiyavākat taṭavi attoyyilaik koṇṭu vanta taṭakkaiyālē, tāṉ virumpiṉa piṭiyai aruḷ ceyyum matayāṉai pōla meym muḻutiṉaiyum taṭavutaluñ ceytāṉ; acceyalāl yāṉ kaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkiya varuttattaip pōkkiṉēṉ eṉṟāḷ.” (kuṟiñci - 18.) 
talaivaṉum, talaiviyum uṟaḻntu kūṟum pakuti: “ivaṉoruttaṉ, taṉṉōṭu kūṭuṅ kuṟippiṉṟi niṟpāraiyum, tāṉ kūṭuṅ kuṟippuṭaiyaṉāyk kaiyālē valitiṟ piṭittukkoḷḷum, ātalāl, ēe! nāṇilaṉāyiruntāṉ” eṉat talaivi kūṟiṉāḷ; atu kēṭṭa talaivaṉ, ‘akkuṟippāl nikaḻvaṉavellām nī aṟivāy; yāṉ aṟiyēṉ. melliya pūṅ kottukkaḷaiyuṭaiya koṭiyai yoppāy! niṉ mēṉi pullutaṟku iṉitāyiruttalāl niṉṉai yāṉ pulliṉēṉ’ eṉṟāṉ; eṉṟalum talaivi, ‘ēṭā! tamakku iṉitāyirukku meṉṟu karutip piṟarkku iṉṉāvaṟṟai valitiṟ ceyvatu iṉpattait tarumō?’ eṉṟāḷ; ataṉaik kēṭṭa talaivaṉ, ‘cuṭart toṭī! niṉ mutukkuṟaiviṉai īṇṭup pōṟṟātu kaiviṭu; yāṉ kūṟukiṉṟataṉaip pēṇik kēḷ; nīruṇpār cilar, vēṭkaiyuṟṟa tamakku aḵtu iṉitāyiṉ uṇpatallatu, annīrkku iṉitāyirukkumeṉṟu uṇparō? uṇṇār kāṇ’ eṉṟāṉ” eṉpatu. (kuṟiñci - 26)
‘tiruntiḻāy kēḷāy’ eṉṉum iṟutippāṭṭil tōḻi puṉainturai vakaiyāṟ paṭaittu moḻiyun  tiṟaṉum, āṇṭut tōṉṟum cuvaiyumellām paṭittaṟintiṉpuṟaṟ pālaṉa.
iṉi, talaiviyum tōḻiyum vaḷḷaippāṭṭilē, nel mutaliya kuṟṟum iyalpu, ‘muṟṟi vaḷainta tiṉaik katirai yuruvic cantaṉa marattāṟ ceyta uralilē peytu, yāṉaik kompāṟ ceyta ulakkaiyāṟ kuṟṟi, talaivaṉai viḷittup pāṭuvēm’ (4) eṉṟum, ‘mūṅkiliṉ nellaip pāṟaiyākiya uralilē peytu, yāṉaik kompu ulakkaiyākak kuṟṟi, nalla cēmpiṉilai cuḷakākap puṭaittu, pāṭuvēmāka’ (5) eṉṟum, ‘aivaṉamākiya veṇṇellaip pāṟaiyākiya uralilē corintu, puliyaik koṉṟa yāṉaiyiṉ kompālum, cantaṉa marattālum ceyta ulakkaikaḷālē kuṟṟi, malai poruntiya nāṭṭai yuṭaiyāṉaip pāṭuvēm’ (7) eṉṟum palapaṭiyāka aṇinturaikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
iṉi, itil, talaivaṉ paṇpum, talaivi paṇpumākak kūṟiyuḷḷa ciṟcila toṭarkaḷ aṟintu makiḻaṟpālaṉa. talaivaṉ, talaivi eṉpōr āciriyaṉāṟ paṭaittuk koḷḷap peṟṟōrē yeṉiṉum, akkālattu naṉ makkaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷē aṅṅaṉam avarkaḷ mēlvaittu viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu orutalai. 
talaivaṉ paṇpu, 
‘	taṉṉuṟṟār iṭukkaṇ ṭavirppāṉ’ 
eṉṟum,
‘	ōrvuṟ ṟorutiṟa molkāta nērkō
laṟampuri neñcat tavaṉ’
eṉṟum,
	                                        ‘ taṉmalai
nīriṉuñ cāya luṭaiyaṉ nayantōrkkut
tērīyum vaṇkai yavaṉ’
eṉṟum,
‘	añcuva tañcā aṟaṉili yallaṉ’
eṉṟum,
                                              ‘pakaiyeṉil
kūṟṟam variṉun tolaiyāṉṟaṉ naṭṭārkkut
tōṟṟalai nāṇā tōṉ’
eṉṟum,
‘	māṟukoṇ ṭāṟṟā reṉiṉum piṟarkuṟṟam
kūṟutal tēṟṟātōṉ’
eṉṟum,
                                            ‘iṭumpaiyāl
iṉmaiyuraittāṟ katuniṟaikka lāṟṟākkāl
taṉmey tuṟappāṉ!’
eṉṟum,
                                                ‘ulakam
purappāṉ pōlvatōr matukaiyu muṭaiyaṉ
vallārai vaḻipaṭ ṭoṉṟaṟin tāṉpōl
nallārkaṭ ṭōṉṟu maṭakkamu muṭaiyaṉ
illōr puṉkaṇ īkaiyiṟ ṟaṇikka
vallāṉ pōlvatōr vaṉmaiyu muṭaiyaṉ’
eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
   talaivi paṇpu,
‘arumaḻai taral vēṇṭil tarukiṟkum perumaiyaḷ’
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
muṉ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya, ‘taṉmalai nīriṉuñ cāyaluṭaiyaṉ nayantōrkkut tērīyum vaṇkaiyavaṉ’ eṉṉum toṭarum,
nīyē, vaḷiyiṉ ikaṉmikun tērum kaḷiṟum,
taḷiyiṟ ciṟantaṉai vanta pulavarkku, 
aḷiyōṭu kaitūvalai.
eṉṉum toṭarum, vēṟucila toṭarkaḷum, kapilar, tam arum peṟal naṇpaṉākiya vēḷpāriyai uḷattiṟ koṇṭu pāṭiyaṉa vākal vēṇṭum. eṉṉai? pāri paṟampiṟ paṉiccuṉait teṇṇīrum, avaṉatu māriyaṉaiya vaṇmaiyum, tērk koṭaiyum vēṟu palliṭattum eṭutturaikkappaṭutaliṉ eṉka.
maṟṟu ivar, ‘pūntaṇṭārp pular cāntiṟ ṟeṉṉavaṉuyar kūṭal’ eṉṟum, ‘poḻipeyal vaṇmaiyāṉ acōkan taṇkā viṉuḷ’ eṉṟum kūṟuvaṉa, āriyappaṭai kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ pōlvāṉōr pāṇṭiyaṉ appoḻutu vaḷḷiyōṉāy viḷaṅkiṉaṉ eṉpatu kuṟippaṉavām.
‘	maṉṉā vulakattu maṉṉiyatu puraimē’
eṉavum,
‘	īta lirantāṟkoṉ ṟāṟṟātu vāḻtaliṟ
cātaluṅ kūṭumō maṟṟu’
eṉavum pōnta toṭarkaḷum iṅkē aṟitaraṟ pālaṉa.
iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭiya toṭarkaḷil palavaṟṟiṉ karuttukkaḷ piṉvarum kuṟaṭpākkaḷilum oruvāṟu amain tiruttal kāṇka.
‘	uṭukkai iḻantavaṉ kaipōla vāṅkē
yiṭukkaṇ kaḷaivatā naṭpu.’
‘	camaṉceytu cīrtūkkuṅ kōlpō lamaintorupāṟ
kōṭāmai cāṉṟōrk kaṇi. ’
‘	añcuva tañcāmai pētaimai yañcuva
tañca laṟivār toḻil. ’
 ‘	kūṟṟuṭaṉṟu mēlvariṉuṅ kūṭi yetirniṟkum
āṟṟa latuvē paṭai.’
‘	tuṉṉiyār kuṟṟamun tūṟṟu marapiṉār
eṉṉaiko lētilār māṭṭu.’
‘	cātali ṉiṉṉāta tillai yiṉitatūum
īta liyaiyāk kaṭai. ’
‘	teyvan toḻāaḷ koḻunaṟ ṟoḻuteḻuvāḷ
peyyeṉap peyyu maḻai.’
iṉi, tamar varaivu maṟuttuḻit, tōḻi mutaliyōr aṟattoṭu niṟkuṅkāl, ‘immalaiyil vāḻvār, muṉ uyirutavi purinta talaivaṟkut talaiviyaik koṭukka niṉaiyātu notumalarkkuk koṭukka niṉaintu, aṟam allaṉa virumpi yoḻukutaliṉ, iṉi, ivviṭattu vaḷḷiyum kiḻaṅku viḻā; malai micaiyil tēṉum toṭā; kollaiyil tiṉaikaḷum katirvāṅki yīṉā’ eṉṟum, ‘malaiyiṭattuk kuṟavar maṭamakaḷir, tām tam kaṇavaraip piḻaiyārāy, avarē teyvameṉṟu vaṇaṅki yeḻutalāl, avaruṭaiya aiyaṉmār tāmum tām toṭutta ampukaḷ piḻaiyārāyiṉār’ eṉṟum kūṟiyiruppaṉa viyakkaṟpālaṉa. vēṭṭamāṭun toḻiluṭaiya malaivāḻnar makaḷir, ittuṇai uṟutiyāṉa kaṟpuṭaiyarāyum, aṟattiṉ nuṭpaṅkaḷai aṟintōrāyum iruntāreṉṉil, innāṭṭu makaḷiratu perumaiyai yāteṉṟu uraikkalākum?
iṉi, itil ciṟumakaḷir taittiṅkaḷil nīrāṭutalum, piṟar maṉaiyiṉ kaṇṇē ceṉṟu, pāṭi, aiyamēṟṟu, āṇṭup peṟṟa vaṟṟaip piṟarkku aḷittalum, kuḻa makaṉai (pommaiyai) vaḷarttu, avaṉukkup peṇ kōṭaṟkuc ciṟucōṟu camaittu, āyattiṟkiṭṭu makiḻtalum eṉṉum mūvakai nōṉpukaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. 
‘nalkūrntār celvamakaḷ’ eṉṟu talaivi arumai pārāṭṭik kūṟappaṭukiṟāḷ. ‘piḷḷaiyāl miṭippaṭṭāruṭaiya celvattaiyuṭaiya makaḷ’ eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷām.
‘neṟiyaṟi ceṟikuṟi puritiri paṟiyā vaṟivaṉ’ eṉṟu kaṇi kūṟappaṭukiṟāṉ. ‘nūṉeṟiyālē anta iruvarum kūṭutaṟ kētuvākiya muḻuttam vēṟupaṭṭut tapputalai, orukālattum aṟiyāta kaṇi’ (cōtiṭaṉ) eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷām. 
‘pariyatu kūrṅkōṭṭa tāyiṉum yāṉai verūum pulitāk kuṟiṉ’ eṉṟu tamiḻ maṟai kūṟutaṟku vēṟāka, vēḻam puliyiṉaip pōr tolaitta ceyti palkāṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
‘eṉ ceyvām’ eṉṟārkkup ‘poṉceyvām’ eṉpatu ōr nākaik kuṟippu moḻiyākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. 
mey toṭṭuc cūḷuṟutal kūṟiyuḷḷatu.
kayilaimalaiyilē, iṟaivaṉ iṟaiviyōṭirukka, irāvaṇaṉ ataṉai yeṭukkaluṟṟatum, yāṉaikaḷ pūvōṭu kūṭiya  nīrai mēlē coriyā niṟkac centāmarai malariṉ akavitaḻil tirumakaḷ vīṟṟiruppatum, nūṟṟuvar talaivaṉākiya turiyōtaṉaṉai, vīmaṉ kuṟaṅkilaṭittu uyir cekuttatum, tirumāl mallaratu maṟattaik keṭuttatum ākiya purāṇa vitikāca kataikaḷum, vāraṇavāci yeṉṉum ūriluḷḷāratu aruḷum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
itiluḷḷa tiricoṟkaḷaik kāṇuṅkāl, ivvāciriyar, coṟkaḷait tam vaḻippaṭuttu āṇṭuḷār eṉpatu pulaṉām. ‘uraittaikkāṇ, pāṭittai, citaittatai, kēṭṭī, varuvatai, iṟantīvāy, ikattantāy, aṟintī yātu, pōttanta, ikattara, iḵtottaṉ, maṟātīvāḷ, vantai, kaṇṭai, tiṉṟi, koṇṭī, eṉpaṉa kāṇka. ivai muṟaiyē uraittukkāṇ, pāṭu, citaittatu, kēḷ, varuvatu, iṟappāy, ikantāy, aṟiyātu, pōtal tanta, ikakka, ivaṉoruttaṉ, maṟāḷ, vā, kāṇ, tiṉ, koḷ;’ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tiripākum. 
piṉvarum pāṭṭukkaḷiluḷḷa iyaṟkai naviṟciyum, piṟa aḻakum ōrntu koḷka.
koccakavuṟuppu
‘	iṭiyumiḻ piraṅkiya viravuppeya ṉaṭunāṭ
koṭiviṭu piruḷiya miṉṉuccey viḷakkattup 
piṭiyoṭu mēyuñ ceypuṉ yāṉai
aṭiyotuṅ kiyakkaṅ kēṭṭa kāṉavaṉ
neṭuvarai yāciṉip paṇavai yēṟik
kaṭuvicaik kavaṇaiyiṟ kalkai viṭutaliṉ
iṟuvarai vēṅkaiyi ṉoḷvī citaṟi
āciṉi meṉpaḻa maḻintavai yutirāt
tēṉce yiṟāal taḷaipaṭap pōki
naṟuvaṭi māviṉ paintuṇa ruḻakkik
kulaiyuṭai vāḻaik koḻumaṭal kiḻiyāp
palaviṉ paḻattiṟ ṟaṅku malaikeḻu veṟpaṉaip 
pāṭukam vāvāḻi tōḻinaṟ ṟōḻi pāṭuṟṟu ’	(5)
‘	imayavil vāṅkiya vīrñcaṭai yantaṇaṉ
umaiyamarn tuyarmalai yiruntaṉa ṉāka
aiyiru talaiyi ṉarakkar kōmāṉ
toṭipoli taṭakkaiyiṟ kīḻpukut tammalai
eṭukkal cellā tuḻuppavaṉ pōla
uṟupuli yuruvēyppap pūtta vēṅkaiyaik
kaṟuvukoṇ ṭataṉmutaṟ kuttiya matayāṉai
nīṭiru viṭarakañ cilampak kūyttaṉ
kōṭupuyk kallā tuḻakku nāṭa kēḷ!
	āriṭai yeṉṉāynī yaravañcāy vantakkāl
nīraṟṟa pulamēpōṟ pulleṉṟāḷ vaikaṟai
kārpeṟṟa pulamēpōṟ kaviṉpeṟu makkaviṉ
tīrāmaṟ kāppatōr tiṟaṉuṇṭē luraittaikkāṇ!
	iruḷiṭai yeṉṉāynī yiravañcāy vantakkāṟ
poruḷillā ṉiḷamaiyōṟ pulleṉṟāḷ vaikaṟai
aruḷvallā ṉākkampō laṇipeṟu mavvaṇi
teruḷāmaṟ kāppatōr tiṟaṉuṇṭē luraittaikkāṇ!
	maṟantiruntā reṉṉāynī malaiyiṭai vantakkāl
aṟañcārāṉ mūppēpō laḻitakkāḷ vaikaṟai
tiṟañcērntā ṉākkampōṟ ṟiruttaku mattirup 
puṟaṅkūṟṟut tīrppatōr poruḷuṇṭē luraittaik kāṇ!
	eṉavāṅku,
	niṉṉuṟu viḻumaṅ kūṟak kēṭṭu
varumē tōḻi naṉmalai nāṭaṉ 
vēṅkai viriviṭa nōkki
vīṅkiṟaip paṇaittōḷ varaintaṉaṉ koḷaṟkē’ 	(2)
6. maṟṟaiya akappakutikaḷ
naṟṟiṇai
eṭṭuttokaiyuḷ oṉṟākiya innūl kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu uṭpaṭa nāṉūṟṟoru akavaṟpākkaḷaiyuṭaiyatu; akattiṉ pakutikaḷaiyē poruḷāka vuṭaiyatu. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷellām oṉpataṭic ciṟumaiyum, paṉṉiraṇṭaṭip perumaiyum uḷḷaṉa. itil irupatu pāṭṭuk kapilar pāṭiyaṉa.
ippākkaḷil, talaimakkaḷiṉ kēṇmaikku, tāmaraiyiṉ taṇṇiya tātiṉaiyūtic cantaṉamarattil vaikkappaṭṭa tēṉum; talaivi, talaivaṉaiyiṉṟi yamaiyāmaikku, ulakam nīraiyiṉṟi yamaiyāmaiyum; talaiviyiṉ cevvari paranta kaṇkaḷukku, ēṉal kāvalar vilaṅkiṉai yeytu paṟitta pakaḻiyum; vēṅkaiyiṉ malarutirtaṟku, kollaṉ ulaikkaḷattu aṭikkum irumpiṉ poṟi citaṟutalum; malaikku, māyōṉum; malaippakkattu oḻukum aruvikku, vāliyōṉāṉa palatēvaṉum; kaḷiṟṟi yāṉai kāṇuntōṟum polintu viḷaṅkutaṟku, pukaḻuṇṭāka vāḻvāruṭaiya celvam politalum; kaṭuñciṉattuṭaṉ pōrceyta valimikka yāṉaikku, murukavēḷum; vāḻai appoḻutu īṉṟa kūriya mukaikku, yāṉaiyiṉ kuruti paṭinta kompum; vāḻaippūvukku, makaḷiratu kūntalum; talaivaṉatu veytuyirppukku, paṭukkaiyilē kiṭanta yāṉaiyiṉ uyirppum; ñeṇṭiṉ kaṇṇukku, nocciyiṉ kariya arumpum; ñeṇṭiṉ kūṭṭam ñāḻaliṉ utirnta malaraik kālāl varittuk kōlañ ceytaṟku, makaḷir veyililē kāyun tiṉaiyaik kaiviralālē tuḻavutalum; mīṉait tiṉṉumāṟu vantu kūṭum puṭkaḷiṉ kuralukku, ciṟiya nāvaiyuṭaiya maṇiyiṉ teḷinta ōcaiyum; nīr vaṟṟiya cēṟṟiluḷḷa koḻuviya mīṉai nukartaṟ poruṭṭu maṇaṉ mēṭṭilē kūṭiyirukkum nārai kaḷukku, aracaratu paṭait tokutiyum; kāṟṟāl alaikiṉṟa mukilukku, nūṟkiṉṟa nuṇṇiya pañcum uvamikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
talaivi yāṉavaḷ pāriyiṉ paṟampumalai pōlap peruṅkavi ṉeytiṉamaiyāl, ariya kāvalai yuṭaiya ḷāyiṉāḷ eṉṟum; talaiviyiṉ aḻaku, muḷḷur maṉṉaṉākiya malaiyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāri pariyēṟic ceṉṟu; iravilē aṭittuk koṇṭuvanta āṉiraikkuriyōr avaṉō ṭetirttup pōr muṉaiyil aḻintāṟpōl, aḻintatu eṉṟum; kollimalait talaivaṉākiya valvillōriyaik koṉṟa tirumuṭikkāriyāṉavaṉ, uṭaṉē avvōriyiṉ  perun teruvilē pukuntataik kaṇṭa ōriyaic cārntār yāvarum oru cēra niṉṟu, pēriraiccaliṭṭāṟ pōla, ivvūrilē kalleṉṉum nakaiyoliyuṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉṟum; kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. kiṭaṅkil eṉṟa ūrum ciṟappikkap peṟṟuḷatu.
‘āḷil peṇṭir tāḷiṟ ceyta, nuṇaṅku nuṇpaṉuval’ eṉṉum toṭar niṉaivu kūrattakkatu. kaṇavaṉai yiḻanta kaimmai makaḷir tam uyirvāḻkkaikku vēṇṭum poruḷiṉ poruṭṭu muyaṉṟu nūṟkiṉṟa mika nuṇṇiya pañcu eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ. puṟappāṭṭilum, ‘paruttip peṇṭiṉ paṉuval’ eṉṟu kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu. akkālattu illiliruntu makaḷirum purintu ciṟappeytiya ittoḻil ippoḻutu nam nāṭṭil arukiṉatu peritum iraṅkattakkatu. malaiyiṉiṭattē tuyil kiṉṟa piṭiyiṉ pakkattil mēkam maṟaittalāl, āṇyāṉai ataṉaik kāṇappeṟātu piḷiṟā niṟkum eṉṟum; malaic cāralil uḻutoḻil puriyum kuṟavaratu iḷamakaḷir kariya viralaiyuṭaiya mantiyai viruntāka ēṟṟuk koṇṭu, kuṭam pōlum palāppaḻattiṉ cuḷaiyai ataṟku aḷippar eṉṟum kūṟiyiruppaṉa viyakkaṟ pālaṉa. kāṭṭakattu vēṭṭuvaṉ peṟum uṇavup poruḷkaḷum (59), talaivi kiḷiyait tūtu viṭutalum (376) naṉṟākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiluḷḷa uḷḷuṟai yuvamaṅkaḷellām viyakkaṟ pālaṉavē.
‘nīriṉṟamaiyā vulakam pōla’, ‘periyōr nāṭi naṭpiṉallatu - naṭṭu nāṭārtam moṭṭiyōr tiṟattē’ eṉṉum toṭarkaḷ,
‘	nīriṉ ṟamaiyā tulakeṉiṉ yāryārkkum
vāṉiṉ ṟamaiyā toḻukku.’
‘	nāṭātu naṭṭaliṟ kēṭillai naṭṭapiṉ
vīṭillai naṭpāḷ pavarkku.’
eṉṉum kuṟaṭpākkaḷiṉ karuttait taḻuviyuḷḷaṉa.
niṉṟa collar nīṭutōṉ ṟiṉiyar
eṉṟu meṉṟōḷ piripaṟi yalarē
tāmarait taṇṭā tūti mīmicaic 
cāntiṟ ṟoṭutta tīntēṉ pōlap
puraiya maṉṟa puraiyōr kēṇmai
nīriṉ ṟamaiyā vulakam pōlat
tammiṉ ṟamaiyā nannayan taruḷi
naṟunutal pacatta lañcic
ciṟumai yuṟupavō ceypaṟi yalarē.
kuṟuntokai
eṭṭuttokaiyuḷ oṉṟākiya innūl kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu uṭpaṭa nānūṟṟiraṇṭu akavaṟpākkaḷaiyuṭaiyatu. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷellām nāṉkaṭic ciṟumaiyum, eṭṭaṭip perumaiyum uḷḷaṉa. itil kapilar pāṭiyaṉa irupattoṉpatu pāṭṭu.
ivaṟṟil, ciṟumalaikku, yāṉaiyum; nāraiyiṉ ciṟiya paciya kālukku, tiṉaiyiṉ tāḷum; malaiyiṉiṉṟu iḻiyum aruvikku, iṟṟi marattiṉatu kalliṭattē paraviya veḷḷiya vērum; 1talaivaṉ perumūccukku, pātiyiravil tuyilum yāṉaiyiṉ uyirppum; tāmarai yilaikku, kaḷiṟṟiṉ ceviyum uvamaiyāka vantuḷḷaṉa. ciṟiya kōṭṭil periya paḻam tūṅkiṉāṟ pōṉṟu, talaiviyiṉ uyir mikac ciṟitu, avaḷuṟṟa kāmam mikap peritu eṉṟum; talaiviyiṉ nīraiyotta cāyal, talaivaṉatu tīyaiyotta maṉavaliyaik keṭuttiṭṭatu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. talaivikkuk kolli malaiyiluḷḷa pāvai uvamikkap paṭṭuḷatu. miḷakiṉ ṟaḻaiyaik kuraṅku aruntum eṉṟu kūṟiyirukkiṟatu. maṟṟum, ivvāciriyar, ‘kēṭuṟṟaviṭattuc ceyta utaviyai aracurimaic ciṟappeyti maṟanta maṉṉaṉ pōla naṉṟi maṟantaṉaiyāyiṉ’ eṉṟu tōḻi talaivaṟkuk kūṟuva
tākavum; ‘talaivaṉatu kēṇmaiyāl vanta iṉṉāmaiyiṉum tuṟakkaviṉpam iṉitāmō’ eṉṟum, ‘aṉṉai ceyta utavikku uyar nilaiulakamum āṟṟutaṟ karitu’ eṉṟum talaivi tōḻikkuk kūṟuvatākavum kūṟiyuḷḷār. ciṟukuṭi pacippiṉ yāṉaiyiṉ kompukaḷai viṟṟu uṇpikkum ōri yeṉpāṉatu kollimalai eṉkiṉṟār. malaiyaṉatu muḷḷūrk kāṉattaip palaviṭattuc ciṟappit tōtukiṟār. ivar kūṟiya, ‘kāṉamaññai yaṟaiyīṉ muṭṭai, veyilāṭu mucuviṉ kuruḷai yuruṭṭuṅ, kuṉṟanāṭaṉ’ eṉpataṉ karuttu,‘vantāy pavaraiyillāmayiṉ muṭṭai yiḷaiyamanti, pantā ṭirumpoḻiṟ palvarai nāṭaṉ’ eṉa māṇikkavācakap perumāṉāl tiruc ciṟṟampalak kōvaiyāril eṭuttāḷap peṟṟatu.
aiṅkuṟunūṟu
eṭṭut tokaiyuḷ oṉṟākiya innūl akattiṉ pakuti
yākiya marutam, neytal, kuṟiñci, pālai, mullai eṉṉum tiṇaikaḷ ovvoṉṟaṉaiyum paṟṟit taṉittaṉi kūṟum nūṟu nūṟu akavaṟ pākkaḷai yuṭaiyatu. ivvaintu nūṟṟaiyum iyaṟṟiṉār muṟaiyē ōrampōkiyār, ammūvaṉār, kapilar, ōtalāntaiyār, pēyaṉār eṉpōr.1 maṟṟait tokaikaḷil pōlavē, pāratam pāṭiya peruntēvaṉāriyaṟṟiya kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu itil mutaṟkaṇ uḷḷatu. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷellām mūṉṟaṭic ciṟṟaḷavum, āṟaṭip pēraḷavum uḷḷaṉa. itaṉ ovvoru nūṟum pappattup pākkaḷ olvoru taṉmaiyiṉavāka iyaṉṟuḷḷaṉa. kapilar pāṭiya kuṟiñcip pakutiyil uḷḷa pattukkaḷ, muṟaiyē aṉṉāy vāḻip pattu, aṉṉāyp pattu, ammavāḻip pattu, teyyōp pattu, veṟippattu. kuṉṟakkuṟavaṉ pattu, kēḻaṟ pattu, kurakkuppattu, kiḷḷaippattu, maññaippattu eṉpaṉavākum. ivaṟṟiṉ ivvoru pāṭṭilum ‘aṉṉāy vāḻi’ eṉpatu mutaliya coṟkaḷēṉum, avaṟṟiṉ pariyāyac coṟkaḷēṉum varutaliṉ, ivai ippeyariṉa vāyiṉa. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷellām coṟcuvai, poruṭcuvai tuvaṉṟik kaṟpārkkuk kaḻipēriṉimai payappaṉa. iyaṟkai vaḷaṅkaḷai yeḻil peṟa viḷakkuvaṉa.
aṉṉāy vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉainam paṭappait
tēṉmayaṅku pāliṉu miṉiya vavarnāṭ
ṭuvalaik kūvaṟ kīḻa
māṉuṇ ṭeñciya kaliḻi nīrē.
ivvoru pāṭṭiliruntē innūliṉ arumai, perumai kaḷait terintu koḷḷalākum. talaivaṉuṭaṉ avaṉūrkkuc ceṉṟu mīṇṭa talaimakaḷ, ‘nī ceṉṟa nāṭu evviyalpiṟṟu?’ eṉa viṉāviya tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu itu. itaṉ poruḷ: ‘aṉṉaiyē! vāḻvāyāka! yāṉ collu mitaṉai virumpik kēḷ. aṉṉai! nam malaip pakkattu iṟāliṉiṉṟum piḻin teṭutta tēṉ kalanta pāliṉuṅ kāṭṭil, nam talaivar nāṭṭilē taḻaikaḷ viḻuntu aḻukik kiṭakkum ciṟu kuḻikaḷiṉ kīḻiṭattavāy, vilaṅkukaḷāl uṇṇappaṭṭu eñciyuḷḷa kalaṅkaṟ ciṉṉīrkaḷ eṉakku mikka iṉimaiyuṭaiyaṉa kāṇ.’ eṉṟu talaivi kūṟiṉāḷ eṉpatu. talaivaratu nāṭu ituvē pōṟ ciṟantatu eṉṟēṉum, itaṉiṉum ciṟantatu eṉṟēṉum talaivi kūṟiyiruppiṉ, avaṭkut talaivaṉ pāluḷḷa aṉpu oru ciṟitum viḷaṅkuva tiṉṟu. ‘talaivaratu nāṭu evviyalpiṟṟu eṉiṉum, atuvē eṉakkuc ciṟantatāvatu’ eṉṟu kūṟuvatē talaiviyiṉ aṉpiṉ tiṟaṉaip pulappaṭuppatākum. ataṉaiyē, ‘avar nāṭṭu uvalaik kūvaṟ kīḻa māṉuṇṭu eñciya kaliḻinīr nam paṭappait tēṉ mayaṅku pāliṉum iṉiya’ eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉāḷ. taḻaikaḷ aḻukik kiṭakkum cēṟṟiṭattil, vilaṅkukaḷ uṇṇak kalaṅkiya ciṟiya nīrai, iṟappa iḻintārum  ettuṇai nīr vēṭkaiyuṟṟa poḻutum uṇṇutaṟku aruvarā niṟpar. uṇṇiṉ, aḵtu appoḻutu iṉimai ciṟitum ilatātalē yaṉṟip piṉpu pala piṇiyum uṇṭākkip peruntuṉpattai viḷaippatumākum. tēṉum, pālum taṉittaṉi mikka iṉimai yuṭaiyavāy, ettuṇaip periyārālum virumpi yuṇṇappaṭuvaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu maṟṟaiyatuṭaṉ kalakkappeṟiṉ, akkalavai parukutaṟku ataṉ mēlum iṉimaiyuṭaittākum. avvaḷavaṉṟi, aḵtu uṭampiṟku uṟuti payantu, piṟ poḻutukaḷil iṉpam viḷaippatu mākum. ‘kalaṅkaṟ ciṉṉīr teruḷāmaiyāl uṇpāṉ’ eṉṉum iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷuraiyum, ‘pāloṭu tēṉ kalantaṟṟē’ eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷum iṅku niṉaikkaṟpālaṉa.  ivvāṟṟāl, yāvarāṉum veṟukkattakum kalaṅkal nīr, yāvarāṉum virumpat takum tēṉpāṟ kalavaiyiṉum talaimakaṭku iṉimai yuṭait tāyiṟṟu. itaṟku eṉṉai kāraṇam? aṉpu eṉpatoṉṟu ettuṇai yiḻinta poruḷaiyum uyarnta poruḷākka valla maruntākavuḷḷatu.
‘	ceruppaṭiyav viḷamparuvac cēyaṭiyiṟ ciṟappuṭaittāl.’ 
‘	eṉakkavaṉṟaṉ vāyumiḻum puṉal puṉitam1.’
eṉṟu civaṉ kūṟiṟṟāka vuḷḷa tiruppāṭṭukkaḷum ivvuṇmai terippaṉa vaṉṟē? ‘aṉpu eṉpa toṉṟiṉ taṉmai amararum aṟintataṉṟu’2 eṉṉil, itaṉ perumaiyai yām eṅṅaṉam uraikkēm? talaivaṉatu nāṭṭiluḷḷa teṉpa toṉṟē paṟṟi, avviṭattuḷḷa mika iḻinta poruḷaiyum talaiviyāṉavaḷ piṟaviṭattuḷḷa mikavuyarnta poruḷiṉum uyarntatākak koṇṭāḷ eṉṟāl, avaḷ talaivaṉiṭattu ettakaiya aṉpuṭaiyaḷātal vēṇṭum? tēṉ eṉa vāḷā kūṟātu, ‘nam paṭappait tēṉ’ eṉṟatu, atu piṟaviṭattuḷḷatiṉum iṉitu eṉpatu tōṟṟutaṟku. itaṉāl, kaṟpuṭai makaḷirāvār, tam piṟantakattu ettuṇaip peruñ celvam niṟaintiruppiṉum, ataṉiṉum tām pukkakattu vaṟumaiyai miku mēṉmai yuṭait teṉṟu koḷvar eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. tōḻiyai aṉṉāy eṉṟatu arumai pārāṭṭi. iru muṟai viḷittatu ārvam paṟṟi. vāḻiyeṉṟatu avaḷ uṭaṉ pōkkiṟkut tuṇai purinta utavi paṟṟi. vēṇṭu eṉṟatu tāṉ collum poruḷiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmai paṟṟi. ivvāṟu ivvāciriyar aṉpiṉatu tiṟattai iraṇṭu mūṉṟaṭikaḷāl viḷaṅka vaitta māṭciyai eṅṅaṉam viyanturaikka vallēm?
iṉi, kurakkuppattil, kuraṅkiṉ talaivaṉāṉa kaṭuvaṉ, ciṟiya pirappaṅkōl koṇṭu, māri mokkuḷaip puṭaittu aḻikkum eṉṟum, malaiyiṭattuḷḷa kaṭuvaṉ naṟaikkoṭi koṇṭu karukkoṇṭa iḷamukilaip puṭaikku meṉṟum, kuraṅku taṉ mītiruntu pāytalāl vaḷaintu nimirum mūṅkiṟkōl, mīṉeṟi tūṇṭil pōl tōṉṟum eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷaṉavum; maññaip pattil malarnta vēṅkai marattilirunta mayil, aṇikalaṉ aṇinta makaḷir pōlat tōṉṟum eṉṟum, pūkkoyyum makaḷir pōlat tōṉṟum eṉṟum, tiṉaiyari tāḷilirunta kuruvi eḻuvatu viḻuvatāy varuntuṟac ciṟakai virittu āṭic cellum mayil, pantāṭum makaḷir pōlat tōṉṟum eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷaṉavum paṭittu iṉpuṟaṟ pālaṉa. iṅkuk kūṟiya ‘mīṉeṟi tūṇṭil’ eṉṟa uvamam, ‘kāṉa yāṉai kaiviṭu pacuṅkaḻai, mīṉeṟi tūṇṭiliṉi vakkum’ eṉak kuṟuntokai yuḷḷum (54) pōntuḷatu. matavēḻamāṉatu, kariya malaippāṟaiyaip piṭiyeṉṟu karutit taḻuvum nāṭaṉ eṉpatum, veṟippattil, ‘poyyā marapiṉūr mutuvēlaṉ’ eṉavum, ‘poypaṭu paṟiyāk kaḻaṅkē’ eṉavum kuṟippiṟ kūṟi, veṟiyāṭṭāḷaṉatu (pūcāri) poymmaiyai viḷakkuvaṉavum pōlvaṉa nakaiccuvai payappaṉavākum. ivaḷ tiṉaippuṉam kāttoḻintāl varaival eṉṟa talaivaṟkut tōḻi kūṟuvatāka, ‘koṭicci kiḷiyōṭṭuṅ kuralaik kiḷiyiṉ kuraleṉak karutip piṟaviṭattuḷḷa kiḷikaḷum tiṉaip puṉattiṟ pōntu aḻikkum eṉṟu kāvalai vilakkuvar pōlum. ākalāl, periya malai nāṭaṉē! ivaḷai viraiya varaintu koḷvāyāka.’ eṉṟu viyapput tōṉṟak kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. piṉṉuḷōrum, ‘āyō veṉuṅ kuralaiyō puṉattai yaḻikkiṉṟatē’ eṉa ikkaruttai eṭuttamaittuḷār. talaivi piṉṉāka vantu kaṇ pottiya viṭattu, ‘aḻakiya kāntaṭ pūppōlum kaiyāl eṉ kaṇkaḷaip putaittōyē!’ eṉ neñcam virumpiṉavar nīyalatum uḷar kollō?’ eṉṟu talaivaṉ kūṟiṟṟāka vuḷḷatu iṉpam viḷaikkiṉṟatu. kampanāṭarum, ‘kāraṉṉa kūntaṟ kuyilaṉṉavaḷ kaṇputaippa, āreṉṉa lōṭu maṉaleṉṉa ayirttuyirttāḷ’ eṉa ikkaruttai yeṭuttu 1irāmāyaṇattiṟ patittu vaittār.
talaivaṉ ūrnta kutirai pārppaṉak kuṟumakavupōl kuṭumit talaiyatu eṉavum; acōkiṉ ṟaḻai neyyōṭu picainta uḻuntu māppōlum eṉavum; poṉpōlum tiṉaiyaik kaṭṭaḷai (poṉ uraiyāṇi) pōlum kēḻal māntum eṉavum; avaraiyai niraiyat tiṉṟa manti, paṇṭa vāṇikar paipōlat tōṉṟum eṉavum kūṟiyuḷḷa uvamaikaḷ peritum makiḻcci viḷaippaṉavā kiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟum pala uvamai kaḷum, uḷḷuṟaikaḷum itiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ‘veḷḷa varampiṉ ūḻi’ eṉa itil vantuḷatu. tolkāppiyaṉār, ‘ai am pal eṉa varūum iṟuti’ eṉpataṉāl, tāmarai, veḷḷam, āmpal eṉa eṇṇup peyarkaḷ kūṟiyiruppatu iṅkē karutaṟ pālatu. āṇ kuraṅkiṉai, ‘mantik kaṇavaṉ kallākkaṭuvaṉ’, ‘kuraṅkiṉṟalaivaṉ’ eṉṟiṅṅaṉam itiṟ kūṟiyirukkiṟatu. ‘kuraṅki ṉēṟṟiṉaik kaṭuvaṉeṉṟalum’ eṉat tolkāppiyaṉār vaḻuvamait tiruttalum kāṇka. puliyiṉ ēṟṟai, piṭiyīṉṟa kuḻavi, takar, puruvai, paṉṟik kuruḷai, paṉṟi yoruttal, mantippaṟaḻ, mantip pārppu eṉṟivai mutaliya marapukaḷum, pala vaḻakkaṅkaḷum itiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
akanāṉūṟu
eṭṭut tokaiyuḷ oṉṟākiya innūl, kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu uṭpaṭa nāṉūṟṟoru akavaṟ pākkaḷai yuṭaiyatu. iḵtu, akam eṉavum, akappāṭṭu eṉavum, neṭuntokai eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷiṉ aṭiyaḷavu ciṟumai patiṉmūṉṟu, perumai muppattoṉṟu. ittokaiyiṉ mutal nūṟṟirupatu pākkaḷaik kaḷiṟṟuyāṉai nirai eṉavum, aṭutta nūṟṟeṇpatu pākkaḷai maṇimiṭai pavaḷam eṉavum, iṟuti nūṟu pākkaḷai nittilakkōvai eṉavum āṉṟōr vaḻaṅkuvar. itiṟ kapilar pāṭiyaṉa patiṉeṭṭup pākkaḷ.
ivaṟṟil, vāḻaikkaṉi, palākkaṉikaḷāl cuṉaiyiṭattu niṟainta tēṉai, vēṭkai mikutiyāl nīreṉṟu karutiyuṇṭa kaṭuvaṉ, ayaliluḷḷa miḷaku koṭi vaḷarap peṟṟa cantaṉa marattiṉ mēl ēṟamāṭṭātu malarppaṭukkaiyil kaṇpaṭai koḷḷum eṉṟum; palaviṉ paḻaṅ koḷḷumāṟu kuravar nāṭṭiya kurampai utirnta vēṅkaip pūvāl mūṭap paṭṭiruppataṉaip puliyeṉṟu karuti añcutaluṟṟa vēḻam, maḻai tavaḻum malaiyilē kaḻai maṭiyap pāyntu ōṭā niṟkum eṉṟum; mūṅkiliṉ ṟuḷaiyil kāṟṟup pukum ōcai kuḻalicaiyākavum, aruvi nīr vīḻcciyiṉōcai muḻaviṉ kuralākavum, kalaiyiṉ kuralōcai peru vaṅkiyamākavum, vaṇṭiṉ pāṭṭu yāḻicaiyākavum koṇṭu, mantiyiṉ avai nōkki viyappuṟumpaṭi, mūṅkil vaḷarnta pāṟaiyil acain tāṭum mayil viṟali pōlat tōṉṟum eṉṟum; aruviyiṟ pāynta karuviralai yuṭaiya manti, palāppaḻattait teppamākak koṇṭu, cāraliluḷḷa ūr muṉṟuṟaiyiṟ ceṉṟu cērum eṉṟum; uḷḷuṟai porunta aḻakākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
talaivaṉ varaintu koḷḷa varukiṉṟamaiyait talaivikku aṟivikkaluṟum tōḻiyāṉavaḷ, ‘kuḷirnta kaṇṇum, taḷir pōlum mēṉiyum uṭaiyāḷē! vāṉaḷāviya malaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya nam talaivar varaitaṟ poruṭṭu vantamaiyāl, eṉakku uṇṭākiya makiḻcciyiṉ aḷavu colliṉ, nāṭu vaṟaṅkūravum kalappai toḻiliṉṟit tuñcavum kōṭai mikka pacumaiyaṟṟa kālaiyil, malai pōlum karaikaḷaiyuṭaiya periya kuḷam niṟaiyum paṭiyākap perumaḻai poḻinta vaikaṟaiyil pallōrum uvanta uvakai yellām eṉṉuḷḷattē peytu vaittāṟ pōlum’ eṉṟu taṉ makiḻcciyaip pulappaṭutturaippatākak kūṟiyirukkiṟatu. ‘ēri niṟaintaṉaiya celvaṉ kaṇṭāy’ eṉṟa tēvāram iṅkē karutattakkatu. 
talaimakaṉ ciṟaip puṟattāṉākat talaimakaḷ tōḻik kuraikku miṭattu, ‘tōḻī! nām, avar iruḷiṭai varutaliṉ ētattai yañci akaṉṟu valittiruppavum, eṉatu neñcu eṉṉaiyum niṉṉaiyum kēḷātu kaiyikantu, paṭukuḻi kaḷaiyuṭaiya vaḻiyil iruḷil mitikku miṭattu avaratu taḷarnta aṭiyait tāṅkumāṟu cellā niṉṟatu’ eṉṟatākak kūṟiyirukkiṟatu. ‘karu nāyiṟu pōlpavar kāloṭu pōy, varunā ḷayalē varuvāy maṉaṉē’ eṉṟu kampar kūṟiyatu iṅkē niṉaikkattakkatu.
‘vēṅkaiyiṉatu poṉpōlum pūṅkottukkaḷaic  cūṭi, vaḷainta villait tōḷiṉ mītirutti, palāppaḻattil viḷainta tēṟalai iḷaiyaroṭum mānti, kuṟipiḻaiyāta veṉṟiyaiyuṭaiya nāy piṉṉē vara vēṭṭaikkuc ceṉṟa kuṟavaṉ kuḷaviyiṉ putal kurutiyoṭu poruntumāṟu muḷḷam paṉṟiyai eytu vīḻttum kuṉṟu’ eṉṟu kāṉavaratu vēṭṭai kūṟiyirukkiṟatu.
‘īṉṟu iḷaitta peṇ puliyiṉ pacikku uṇavu tarumāṟu malaik kukaiyiṉiṉṟum puṟappaṭṭa pulippōttu, āmāṉiṉ peṇ alarumpaṭi, āmāṉēṟṟaip pāyntu, valamuṟa vīḻtti, kariya kalliṭamellām civakkum paṭi iḻuttuvarum’ eṉap pulivēṭṭam kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. 
piṟaruṟṟa tuṉpattiṟkup piṟarum varuntuvar tammaiyuṟṟa tuṉpam tamakkō eḷitu eṉṉum poruḷuṭaittāka, 
‘	piṟaruṟu viḻumam piṟarum nōpa
tammuṟu viḻumam tamakkō tañcam ,’
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu.
vaṇṭiṉai, ‘aṟukāl yāḻicaip paṟavai’ eṉṟu kūṟiyiruk kiṟatu. 
irutalaip puḷ pōla iruvarkkum uyir oṉṟākiya kēṇmaiyar eṉṟum; maḻaiyiṟ kalitta picci mukaiyiṉ verin pōlum kaṇ eṉṟum; yāṉaik kayiṟṟup puṟam pōlum kalliṉ mēl ōṅkiya ciṟu neṟi eṉṟum; ēṟṟiṉ imiliṟ pūṭṭiya kayiṟu pōlat talaivaṉatu tiraṇṭa tōḷil aṇinta mālai puraḷa  eṉṟum uvamaikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  naḷḷi yāṉavaṉ, irappōrkku maḻai curantāṟpōl, tērum, kaḷiṟum nalkuvaṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
7. puṟapporuṭ pakutiyum piṟavum
puṟanāṉūṟu
eṭṭut tokaiyuḷ oṉṟākiya innūl kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu uṭpaṭa nāṉūṟṟoru akavaṟ pākkaḷaiyuṭaiyatu. puṟattiṉ pakutiyākiya veṭci mutaliya tiṇaikaḷukkuriya tuṟaip poruḷamainta nāṉūṟu pākkaḷai yuṭaimaiyiṉ puṟanāṉūṟu eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. itu puṟam eṉavum, puṟappāṭṭu eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. innūliṉ iṟutiyiluḷḷa pāṭalkaḷ pala citaintu viṭṭamaiyiṉ, pākkaḷiṉuṭaiya aṭikaḷiṉ ciṟumai perumai terintu koḷḷak kūṭavillai. ‘innūlāl, paṇṭaik kālattu ittamiḻ nāṭṭilirunta cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyar kaḷākiya muṭiyuṭai vēntar, ciṟṟaracar, amaiccar, cēṉait talaivar, vīrar eṉkiṟa palaruṭaiya carittiraṅkaḷum, kaṭaiyeḻu vaḷḷalkaḷiṉ carittiraṅkaḷum, kaṭaic caṅkap pulavar palaruṭaiya carittiraṅkaḷum, akkālattuḷḷāruṭaiya naṭai mutaliyaṉavum, iṉṉum paṟpalavum naṉkup pulappaṭum’ 
itiluḷḷa pākkaḷil, kapilar pāṭiyaṉa irupatteṭṭu. avaṟṟuḷ, iraṇṭu pāṭṭuc celvak kaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉaip pāṭiyavai; tirumuṭikkāriyaip pāṭiyavai nāṉku. pēkaṉaip pāṭiyatu oṉṟu. pāriyaip pāṭiyavai ēḻu. pāri tuñciya vaḻi iraṅkip pāṭiyavai eṭṭu. pāri makaḷiraik koṇṭu ceṉṟu viccikkōṉaip pāṭiyatu oṉṟu. iruṅkōvēḷaip pāṭiyavai iraṇṭu. tām vaṭakkirukkum poḻutu pāriyai viḷittup pāṭiyatu oṉṟu. makaṭpāṟ kāñciyākap potuvē pāṭiyavai iraṇṭu. pāri tuñciyataṟku iraṅkip pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ mikavum urukkamāṉavai.
celvak kaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉ tam kaiyaip paṟṟi, ‘numkai melliya’ eṉak kūṟiyapoḻutu, ‘yāṉai, kutirai, tērkaḷai muṟaiyāṉē celuttiyum, villai nāṇēṟṟi ampiṉai vaḻaṅkiyum pōr puriyavum, paricilarkku ariya aṇikalaṅkaḷai yaḷikkavum niṉ kaikaḷ valiya vākum; ūṉaiyum, tuvaiyaiyum, kaṟiyaiyum, cōṟṟaiyum uṇṭu varuntum ceyalallatu vēṟu ceyal aṟiyāvākaliṉ, niṉṉaip pāṭuvār kaikaḷ meliyavāyiṉa’ eṉṟum; pāri tuñciya piṉ, paṟampiṉai nōkki niṉṟu, ‘perum pukaḻai yuṭaiya paṟampē! matuvuṭaṉē, tuvaiyaiyum, ūṉaiyu muṭaiya cōṟṟai virumpiyavāṟē tarum mikka celvam mutirntu, muṉpu emmoṭu naṭpuc ceytāy; ippoḻutu, pāri iṟantāṉākak kalaṅkic ceyalaṟṟu, nīrvār kaṇṇai yuṭaiyēmāyt toḻutu niṉṉai vāḻttic cellutum’ eṉṟum kūṟiyiruttalāl, ivvāciriyar matuvum, ūṉum uṇṇu miyalpiṉar eṉac cilar karutāniṟpar. mika paḻaiya nāḷil, evarum ivvaḻakkaṅkaḷaik kuṟṟa meṉṟu karutiṟṟilar eṉpataṟku, vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa maṉu mutaliya miruti nūlkaḷē takka cāṉṟākum. eṉiṉum, nallicaip pulavarākiya kapilar ivvaḻakkamuṭaiyār eṉpataṟku yām eḷitil iṇaṅkak kūṭavillai. ivvāciriyar tāmē iṉṉā nāṟpatil ivaṟṟaik kaṭintirukkiṉṟaṉar. perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭaiyil, antaṇarkku vēṟu tūya uṇavu kūṟappaṭṭiruttalum karutaṟpālatu. pulavarkaḷ tammaip pāṇar mutaliyōr pōl vaittup pāṭum vaḻakkamuṭaiyarākaliṉ, kapilarum tammaip pāṇarpōl vaittup pāṭiyuḷḷā reṉak koḷḷutal nēritu eṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu. iṅṅaṉam pāṭuvatuṇ ṭeṉpatu, ciṟukuṭi kiḻāṉ paṇṇaṉaik kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ pāṭiya, ‘yāṉ vāḻunāḷum paṇṇaṉ vāḻiya . . . pacippiṇi maruttuvaṉillam, aṇittō cēyttō kūṟumi ṉemakkē’ eṉṉum puṟap pāṭṭāl naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum. annāḷil, aracar mutaliyōrum, pāṇar pōlvārai iṟappa viḻintārāka votukkātu, avarkaḷ pāl mikka aṉpupārāṭṭi vantirukka vēṇṭum eṉa, itu pōṉṟavaikaḷāl karutappaṭukiṟatu. 
celvakkaṭuṅkōvaip pāṭiya pāṭṭu oṉṟu mikka poruṭciṟappuṭaittā yiruttaliṉ, ataṉaiyum, ataṟku, ‘vēṟupaṭa vanta vuvamat tōṟṟam’ eṉṉum uvamaviyaṟ cūttira vuraiyil, pērāciriyar tam nuṇṇaṟivu māṭciyāṟ kaṇṭu eḻutiya uraiyaiyum īṇṭut tarukiṉṟām.
‘	vaiyaṅ kāvalar vaḻimoḻin toḻukap 
pōkam vēṇṭip potuccoṟ poṟāa
tiṭañciṟi teṉṉu mūkkan turappa
voṭuṅkā vuḷḷat tōmpā vīkaik
kaṭantaṭu tāṉaic cēra lātaṉai
yāṅṅaṉa mottiyō vīṅkucelaṉ maṇṭilam1
poḻuteṉa varaiti puṟakkoṭut tiṟatti
māṟi varuti malaimaṟain toḷitti
yakaliru vicumpi ṉāṉum 
pakalviḷaṅ kalaiyāṟ palkatir virittē’	 (puṟam - 8)
ippāṭṭu, uvamikkappaṭum poruḷākiya cēralātaṉai uvamaiyākkiyum, uvamaiyākiya tiṅkaḷ maṇṭilattaip poruḷākkiyum vaittu, uḷḷuṟai uvamam pōla, uvamaiyaṭai poruḷukkum, poruḷaṭai uvamaikkum maṟuttuk koḷḷumāṟu iyaṟṟappaṭṭuḷateṉak koṇṭu, piṉ varumāṟu viḷakkukiṉṟār pērāciriyar.
“vaiyaṅ kāvalar vaḻimoḻin toḻuka” eṉṟamaiyāl, ivaṉ taṉ ṟaṉmaiyaṉā yoḻukuva taṉṟip piṟa varacar vaḻippaṭa māṭṭāṉ; matiyamāyiṉ, oḻinta kōḷum cellat tāṉum cellum eṉṟum; ‘pōkam vēṇṭip potuc coṟ poṟātu’ eṉṟamaiyāmal, ivaṉ iṉpa nukarcciyiṉ poruṭṭuc ciṟappillāk kaṭṭurai yeytāṉ; matiyamāyiṉ, irupat teḻuvar makaḷiroṭu pōkan tuyttu, ellārkkum oppa aṟañceyyātu, urōkiṇi mēṟ kaḻi peruṅ kātal koṇṭu ciṟappillāk kaṭṭurai yeytum eṉṟum; ‘iṭañ ciṟiteṉṉu mūkkan turappa’ eṉṟamaiyāl, matimaṇṭilam eññāṉṟum taṉ ṉellaik kaṇṇē varum eṉṟum; ‘oṭuṅkā vuḷḷam’ eṉṟamaiyāl, matiyam tēyntu oṭuṅkum eṉṟum; ‘ōmpāvīkai’ eṉṟamaiyāl, matiyamāṉatu nāṭōṟum orōvōr kalaiyākap palluyirkkum iṉpam payakkumāṟu taruvatallatu, uṭaiya vellām oru kālē koṭātu eṉṟum; ‘kaṭantaṭu tāṉai’ eṉṟamaiyāl, matikkut tāṉaiyākiya tārakai yellām pakaik katirākiya pariti maṇṭilattiṟkut tōṟkum eṉṟum; iv vāṟṟāl uvamai yaṭaiyellām etir maṟuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. ‘vīṅku celal maṇṭilam’ eṉavē, matiyam kaṭaiyāyiṉār katiyiṟ cellum; pāṭṭuṭait talaivaṉ talaiyāyiṉār katiyiṟ cellum eṉṟum; ‘poḻuteṉa varaiti’ eṉavē, nāṭōṟum nāḻikai vēṟupaṭṭu eṟitti eṉṟataṉāl, avaṉ poḻutu ceyyāṉ eṉṟum; ‘puṟakkoṭut tiṟatti’ eṉavē, tōṟṟōr pōṉṟu oḷi maḻuṅkic celkiṉṟā yeṉṟataṉāl, avaṉ piṟar tōṟṟōṭak kāyntu cuṭar pōla viḷaṅkuvaṉ eṉṟum; ‘māṟi varuti’ eṉavē, tiṅkaṭōṟum māṟip piṟatti yeṉṟataṉāl, avaṉ oru peṟṟiyē nilai peṟṟāṉ eṉṟum; ‘malai maṟain toḷitti’ eṉavē, malai cārnta vaḻit tōṉṟā yeṉṟataṉāl, avaṉ taṉṉāṭṭu malaiyiṉum mīkkūṟappaṭum eṉṟum; ‘akaliru vicum
piṉāṉum’ eṉavē, avaṉ ivvulakattu nilai pēṟuṭaiyaṉ eṉṟum; ‘pakal viḷaṅkalai’ eṉavē, avaṉ irupoḻutum viḷaṅkum eṉṟum; poruṭ kuriya aṭaiyellām avvāṟē etir maṟuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.” kapilaratu pāṭṭu eṉṉum māṇikkattai matikka vallār pērāciriyarē pōlum!
‘	nallavun tīyavu malla kuviyiṇarp
pullilai yerukka māyiṉu muṭaiyavai
kaṭavuḷ pēṇē meṉṉā vāṅku
maṭavar melliyar celliṉuṅ
kaṭavaṉ pāri kaivaṇ maiyē.’ 	(puṟam - 106)
‘	pāri pāri yeṉṟupala vētti
yoruvaṟ pukaḻvar cennāp pulavar
pāri yoruvaṉu mallaṉ
māriyu muṇṭīṇ ṭulakupurap patuvē.’	 (puṟam - 107)
‘	nāḷaṉṟu pōkip puḷḷiṭai taṭpap 
pataṉaṉṟu pukkut tiṟaṉaṉṟu moḻiyiṉum
vaṟitu peyarkuva rallar neṟikoḷap
pāṭāṉ ṟiraṅku maruvip 
pīṭukeḻu malaiyaṟ pāṭi yōrē.’	(puṟam - 124)
patiṟṟuppattu
eṭṭuttokaiyuḷ oṉṟākiya innūl, muṭiyuṭai vēnta rākiya cērar patiṉmaraic caṅkappulavar patiṉmar pāṭiya nūṟu akavaṟpākkaḷai yuṭaiyatu. pappattup pākkaḷuḷḷa pattup pakutikaḷait tokutta tākaliṉ, itu patiṟṟuppattu eṉap paṭṭatu. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷellām puṟattiṇaikkuriya tuṟaip poruḷamaintaṉa. ovvoru pāṭṭiṟkum avaṟṟiluḷḷa ciṟappuṭaiya toṭarāl peyariṭṭiruppatum, vaṇṇamum, tūkkum eḻutiyiruppatum, ovvoru pattiṉ īṟṟilum pāṭap paṭṭōr varalāṟṟai yuṇarttum patikam kūṟiyiruppatum innūliṉ ciṟappākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru ciṟappuṭaiya toṭarāl pāṭaṭukkaḷukkup peyar vaittiruppatu koṇṭu, paṇṭaiyōr, pāṭṭukkaḷiṉ ovvoru collaiyum naṉku ārāyntu, avaṟṟuṭ ciṟappuṭaiyaṉa kaṇṭu vantaṉar eṉṉum uṇmai pulaṉākiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam peyar mutaliyavum, patikamum eḻutiṉōr iṉṉāriṉṉāreṉat tuṇiyak kūṭavillai eṉiṉum, avai mikka paḻamaiyuḷḷaṉaveṉṟē terikiṟatu. ovvoru patikattiṉ piṉpum, avvaracarkaḷ pāṭiṉōrkku aḷitta paricil iṉṉaveṉṟum, avarkaḷ aracu vīṟṟirunta kālam ittuṇaiya veṉṟum terivikkiṉṟa kaṭṭurait toṭarkaḷ varaiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiliruntu, av varacarkaḷ tamiḻiṉiṭattum, tamiḻp pulavariṭattum ettuṇaip perumatippuṭaiya reṉpatum, ettuṇaip peruvaṇmai yuṭaiya reṉpatum naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa. patiṟṟuppattil, eḻām pattuk kapilar pāṭiyatākum. ataṉāṟ pāṭappaṭṭōṉ celvak kaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉ eṉṉum cērar perumāṉāvaṉ. ēḻām pattiṉ mutaṟpāṭṭiliruntu, vēḷpāri tuñciya piṉpu kapilar celvakkaṭuṅkōvai yaṭuttu itu pāṭiṉāreṉṟu terikiṉṟatu. itiluḷḷa,
pularnta cāntiṟ pularā vīkai
malarnta mārpiṉ māvaṇ pāri
muḻavumaṇ pulara viravala riṉaiya
vārāc cēṭpulam paṭarntōṉ aḷikkeṉa
irakku vārē ṉeñcik kūṟēṉ
ītta tiraṅkā ṉīttoṟu makiḻāṉ
īttoṟu māvaḷ ḷiyaṉeṉa nuvaluniṉ
nallicai taravan ticiṉē
eṉṉun toṭarkaḷ pāriyiṉ arumaiyaiyum, kapilaratu perun taṉmaiyaiyum, celvak kaṭuṅkōviṉ vaṇmaiyaiyum terivittu, uḷḷattaik karaiyac ceykiṉṟaṉa.
celvak kaṭuṅkō vāṉavaṉ antaṇark kallatu paṇitalaṟi yāṉ eṉṟum, naṭṭōrk kallatu kaṇṇañcāṉ eṉṟum, nilantiṟam peyaruṅ kālaiyāyiṉum kiḷanta col poytta laṟiyāṉ eṉṟum, nakaiyiṉum poyyā vāymaiyum, pakaivar puṟañcoṟ kēḷāp puraitī roṇmaiyum uṭaiyaṉ eṉṟum, vēḷviyāṟ ṟēvar kaṭaṉum, kēḷviyāl muṉivar kaṭaṉum, putalvarāṟ pitirar kaṭaṉum celuttiyavaṉeṉṟum, avaṉatu mātēvi kāmar kaṭavuḷumāḷuṅ kaṟpiṉaḷ eṉṟum ippattilē kapilar pārāṭṭik kūṟiyuḷḷār. koṭumaṇam, pantar eṉṟa ūrkaḷum, nēri, ayirai eṉṟa malaikaḷum celvakkōvukku uriyavākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉpamē tuykkum vaṭapulam (uttara kuru) itiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ivvaracar perumakaṉ, māya vaṇṇaṉai maṉaṉuṟap peṟṟa
vaṉeṉṟum, akkaṭavuḷukku okantūr eṉṉum patiyiṉai yaḷittava ṉeṉṟum patikam kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivaṉ irupattainti yāṇṭu aracu vīṟṟiruntā ṉeṉṟum, kapilarukkuc ‘ciṟupuṟameṉa nūṟāyiraṅ kāṇaṅ koṭuttu, naṉṟā veṉṉum kuṉṟēṟi niṉṟu, taṉ kaṇṇiṟ kaṇṭa nāṭellām kāṭṭik koṭuttāṉ’ eṉṟum patikattiṉ piṉṉuḷḷa kaṭṭuraikaḷ terivikkiṉṟaṉa. cēramāṉpāṟ kapilar iṅṅaṉam perum paricil peṟṟa ceyti, iḷañcēralirum poṟaiyaip peruṅkuṉṟūrk kiḻār pāṭiya oṉpatām pattil, ‘kapilaṉ peṟṟa vūriṉum palavē’ eṉṟu uvamaiyil vaittōti yiruntalālum naṉku valiyuṟuttap paṭukiṉṟatu.
iṉṉā nāṟpatu 
patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakkiṉuḷ 1oṉṟāya innūl, kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu uḷpaṭa nāṟpattoru veṇpākkaḷai yuṭaiyatu. itiluḷḷa ovvoru pāṭṭum, iṉṉatu iṉṉatu ‘iṉṉā’ eṉak kūṟutaliṉ, iḵtu iṉṉā nāṟpatu eṉap peya reytiṟṟu. itaṟku etirāka, iṉṉatu iṉṉatu ‘iṉitu’ eṉak kūṟum pākkaḷai yuṭaiya nūl iṉiyavai nāṟpatu eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu maṟṟoṉṟaip pārttup pāṭiyatākum eṉak karutaṟku iṭaṉuṇṭu. patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷ, kaṭaic caṅkap pulavarkaḷālum, ciṟitu piṟkālattu viḷaṅkiya pulavar kaḷālum, iyaṟṟap paṭṭaṉaveṉat terikiṟatu. ivaṟṟuṭ cila nūlkaḷ akap poruḷākiya iṉpattaik kūṟāniṟka, pala nūlkaḷ aṟam mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiya nītikaḷaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avai kūṟum nītikaḷ tām, perumpālum ellā makkaḷum mēṟ koḷḷat takkavāyum, uṟuti payappaṉavāyum uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻiluḷḷa oppuyarvillā nīti nūlkaḷiliruntē tamiḻp perumakkaḷiṉ mēṉmai malaimicai viḷakkup pōl yāvarkkum viḷaṅkā niṟkum. naṭuvu nilaiyuṭaiya mēṉāṭṭup pulavarkaḷum ivvuṇmaiyiṉai aṟintu kūṟi vaittiruk kiṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟirukkavum, tiru. em. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār, M.A. avarkaḷ, tām eḻutiya tamiḻārāycci (Tamil Studies) eṉṟa puttakattil, tamiḻ makkaḷai varampiṉṟip puṟam paḻittuk kūṟi varumiṭaiyē, ‘paḻaṅkālattil tamiḻ makkaḷ oḻukkattil mikat tāḻnta nilaiyiliruntaṉar; ātalāṟṟāṉ tirukkuṟaḷ mutaliya nīti nūlkaḷ pala tōṉṟuvavāyiṉa.’ eṉkiṉṟaṉar. nīti nūl oṉṟu millaiyāyiṉ, akkālattu makkaḷellām oḻukkattiluyarntavar eṉpatu avaratu karuttup pōlum! avaratu tarukka vaṟivutāṉ iruntavā ṟeṉṉē! avar piḻaiyiṉai avaratu neñcamē yeṭuttuk kāṭṭiyiṭai viṭā toṟukkum eṉpatu tuṇipu. 
iṉi, ivvāciriyar, tuṉpiṉ mūlaṅkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉa veṉak kaṇṭu, avaṟṟaiyē tokutteṭuttuk kūṟiyiruppatu pārāṭṭaṟ pālatoṉṟām. makkaḷ yāvarum tām virumpuvatu iṉpamē yāyiṉum iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷiṉ kāraṇaṅkaḷai yaṟintu, ēṟṟam peṟṟi naṭavāmaiyāl, tuṉpamē peritum eytu kiṉṟaṉar. iḵtu ekkālattiṟkum potuvāka okkum, iṉpattiṉ kāraṇattai yaṟintu mēṟkoḷḷutaliṉum, mutaṟkaṭ ceyyaṟ pālatu, tuṉpattiṉ kāraṇattai yaṟintu, ataṉiṉ nīṅku talākum. ātalāṟṟāṉ, antaṇmai pūṇṭa kapilar, tuṉpiṉ kāraṇaṅkaḷait tokutturaippārāyiṉar eṉka.
innūl, tirukkuṟaḷ mutaliyaṉa pōṉṟu kūṟa vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷaiyellām aṭaivupaṭa vakuttuk kūṟa vanta taṉṟu ākaliṉ, itaṉai, avaṟṟōṭu oppiṭṭup pārttal poruntātu. oru pēraṟiñar oruvāṟāka ulakiyalai viraintu nōkkuṅkāl, avaruḷḷattil iṉṉāveṉat tōṉṟiyavaikaḷ, itil avvappaṭiyē vaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉaveṉṟē koḷḷa vēṇṭum. itil, nītikaḷē yallāmal, ciṟcila makkaḷiyaṟkai mutaliyavum kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. orē karuttup palaviṭattil vevvēṟu toṭarkaḷāṟ kūṟappaṭṭu mirukkiṟatu. kaḷḷuṇṭal, kavaṟāṭal, ūṉuṇṭal  eṉpaṉa itiṟ kaṭiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. innūlil vantuḷḷa, ‘ūṉait tiṉṟūṉaip perukkutal muṉṉiṉṉā,’ ‘kuḻavikaḷuṟṟa piṇiyiṉṉā’, ‘kallāruraikkuṅ karumap poruḷiṉṉā’ eṉṉum toṭarkaḷōṭu, iṉiyatu nāṟpatil vantuḷḷa ‘ūṉait tiṉṟūṉaip perukkāmai muṉṉiṉitē’, ‘kuḻavi piṇiyiṉṟi vāḻta liṉitē’, ‘kaṟṟaṟintār kūṟuṅ karumap poruḷiṉitē’ eṉṉum toṭarkaḷ oṟṟumai yuṟutal kāṇka.
poruḷuṇarvā rilvaḻip pāṭṭuraitta liṉṉā iruḷkūr ciṟuneṟit tāntaṉippōk kiṉṉā
aruḷillār taṅkaṭ celaviṉṉā viṉṉā
poruḷillār vaṇmai purivu. 	(10)
mūttanāyaṉār tiruviraṭṭai maṇimālai
ituvum, piṉvarum iraṇṭu pirapantaṅkaḷum, patiṉōrān tirumuṟait tokuppiṟ cērntuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟukkum, caṅkap pulavar pāṭiya eṭṭuttokai, pattuppāṭṭukkaḷukkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumaikaḷ, ‘nakkīrar’ eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.1 11-ām tirumuṟaiyil vantuḷḷa mūtta nāyaṉār tiruviraṭṭai maṇimālai, civaperumāṉ tiruviraṭṭai maṇimālai, civaperumāṉ tiruvantāti yeṉṉum pirapantaṅkaḷ mūṉṟaiyum aruḷic ceyta kapila tēvanāyaṉā reṉpavar ivarēyeṉṟu periyōr kūṟuvar’ eṉṟu makā makōpāttiyāyar, tiru. u.vē. cāminātaiya ravarkaḷum 2‘kapilar ceyta vēṟu pirapantaṅkaḷ cila patiṉōrān tirumuṟaiyiṟ cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa’ eṉṟu, tiru. a nārāyaṇacāmi aiyaravarkaḷum oruvāṟu nekiḻntu kūṟiyuḷḷamaiyum kāṇka. ‘eṉaiyantāti coṉṉavaṉ kapilaṉ’ eṉṟu civapirāṉ kūṟiyatākat tiruviḷaiyāṭal kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itu, veṇpāvum, kaṭṭaḷaik kalittuṟaiyumākac coṟ ṟoṭar nilaiyāy maṇṭalittu varum irupatu pāṭṭukkaḷai yuṭaiyatu. tamiḻil mūtta piḷḷaiyārākiya vināyakak kaṭavuḷaip paṟṟik kūṟuvatil innūlē mutaṉmaiyuṭaiyatu eṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu. itilirunta iraṇṭu ceyyuṭkaḷ iḷampūraṇa vaṭikaḷāl mēṟkōḷāka eṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tiruvākkuñ ceykarumaṅ kaikūṭṭuñ ceñcoṟ
peruvākkum pīṭum perukkum - uruvākkum 
ātalāl vāṉōru māṉai mukattāṉaik
kātalāl kūppuvartaṅ kai. 	(1)
civaperumāṉ tiruviraṭṭai maṇimālai
itu, veṇpāvālum, kaṭṭaḷaik kalittuṟaiyālum civaperumāṉaip pāṭiyatōr pirapantam; coṟṟoṭar nilaiyāka vuḷḷatu. itil muppattēḻu pāṭṭukkaḷ ippoḻutu kāṇappaṭuvaṉa.
aṭiyōmait tāṅkiyō vāṭai yuṭuttō
kuṭiyōmpa mānitiyaṅ koṇṭō - poṭiyāṭu
neṟṟiyūr vāḷarava nīḷcaṭaiyāy niṉṉūrai
yoṟṟiyū rākkiṟ ṟurai.	 (15)
civaperumāṉ tiruvantāti
itu, nēricai veṇpā nūṟuṭaiyatāyc coṟ ṟoṭar nilaiyāy, iṟutip pāṭṭiṉ iṟutic cīr mutaṟpāṭṭiṉ mutaṟ cīrāka maṇṭalittu muṭiyap peṟṟuḷatu. itaṉkaṇ akap poruṭṭuṟaiyamainta pākkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. itiluḷḷa pākkaḷellām, maṭakkum, tiripum poruntiyaṉa. civaperumāṉaip paṟṟiya ivviraṇṭu pirapantaṅkaḷilum tiruvoṟṟiyūr, tirumutukuṉṟu, tiruvaṇṇāmalai, tirukkōvalūr, tiruvalañcuḻi, tiruccattimuṟṟam, tiruvārūr, tiruppaḻaṉam, tiruvaiyāṟu, tirukkoṭumuṭi mutaliya pala tiruppatikaḷ pāṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
‘	ūruma toṟṟiyū ruṇkalaṉum veṇṭalaiyē 
yūrum viṭaiyoṉ ṟuṭaitōlē - yūrum
paṭanāka maṭṭār paṇamālai yītō
paṭanāka maṭṭār paricu. ’	(6)
kapilam
tamiḻiluḷḷa pirapantaṅkaḷukku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟunūl pāṭṭiyal eṉappaṭum. paṉṉiru pāṭṭiyal eṉa vaḻaṅkum nūliluḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷiṉ piṉ eḻutiyuḷḷa peyarkaḷāl, avaṟṟai iyaṟṟiṉōr caṅkap pulavar paṉṉiruva reṉak koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. ippoḻutu paṉṉirupāṭṭiyal eṉa vaḻaṅkuvatu, paṉṉiruvar taṉittaṉi yiyaṟṟiya pāṭṭiyal
kaḷiliruntu ciṟcila cūttiraṅkaḷai yeṭuttut tokuttu vaitta tākal vēṇṭum. atililuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ caṅkap pulavari
yaṟṟiyaṉa veṉpatu tuṇipāyiṉ, ataṉuḷ kapilar peyarum kāṇappaṭuvatāl, avarum pāṭṭiyal iyaṟṟiyuḷḷāreṉṟum, atu kapilam eṉa vaḻaṅkaṟkuriyatākum eṉṟum koḷḷalākum.
muṭipu
itukāṟum ceyta ārāycciyāl, tamiḻ moḻiyai vaḷarttaṟ poruṭṭākap paṇṭai nāḷilē maturaiyampati yiṟṟamiḻc caṅkam naṭaipeṟṟu vantateṉpatum, caṅkattu vīṟṟirunta nallicaip pulavar perumakkaḷuḷḷē kapilarāṉavar kalviyilum, ceyyuḷiyaṟṟuvatilum, vāymai, aṉpu mutaliya kuṇaṅkaḷilum oppuyar villārāy viḷaṅkiṉareṉpatum, avaratu perumaiyai akkālattu vēntarkaḷum pulavarkaḷum oruṅku pārāṭṭi yuḷḷārkaḷeṉpatum, vaḷḷalākiya vēḷpārikku avar āruyirt tōḻarāy viḷaṅkiṉareṉpatum, vēḷpāri tuñciya piṉ avaṉatu piriviṟku āṟṟātu palavāṟu pulampiṉareṉpatum, pārimakaḷirait takutiyuḷḷa āṭavarkku vāḻkkaip paṭutta vēṇṭi mika muyaṉṟaṉareṉpatum, cēramāṉ celvakkaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉ, malaiyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāri, vēḷpēkaṉ mutalāyiṉāraiyum avar pāṭiyuḷḷāreṉpatum, kuṟiñcittiṇai pāṭuvatil nikaraṟṟa āṟṟaluṭaiyavareṉpatum, āriyavaracaṉ pirakattaṉait tamiḻaṟivuṟutta avar pāṭiya kuṟiñcippāṭṭum, kuṟiñcikkaliyum mutaliya ceyyuṭkaḷ, coṟ ceṟivum, poruṭ polivum uṭaiyavāy, aṉaittaḻakum porunti viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa veṉpatum, avaṟṟiliruntē paṇṭai nāḷil tamiḻ aṭaintirunta mēṉmaiyum, tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ uḷḷap perumaiyum naṉku veḷiyākiṉṟaṉaveṉpatum, piṟavum teḷivuṟuttap peṟṟamai kāṇka.
vāḻka maṉṉavaṉ vāḻka maṉṉuyir 
vāḻka centamiḻ vāḻka cemmaiyē.


akattiyar
varalāṟu

3. akattiyar varalāṟu
tamiḻp pulavarkaḷāka ippoḻutu aṟiyappaṭuvōr yāvariṉum kālattāl muntiyavarum, iyal icai nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻukkum ciṟantatōr ilakkaṇañ cey taruḷiyum, tamiḻc  caṅkam niṟuviyum, naṉmāṇākkar palarkku nūlaṟivuṟuttiyum tamiḻmoḻiyaip pōṟṟi vaḷartta perumaiyil mutaṉmai yuṟṟiruppōrum āciriyar akattiya ṉārāvar. akattiyar vaṭakkiṉiṉṟum pōntavareṉap pala kataikaḷum kūṟāniṟpa, avar teṟkiṉkaṇ iruntavarēyeṉa orucārār kūṟupa. 'ceṅkōṉṟaraic celavu' eṉṉum ōr paḻaiya nūlil ciṟappuppāyiram pōṟ kāṇappaṭum oru ceyyuḷ 'paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟut talaippāyccal  ēḻ teṅka nāṭṭu muttūr akattiyaṉ kūṟṟu' eṉa avvurai kūṟukiṉṟatu . akattiyar vaṭakkiṉiṉṟum pōntavarāka, teṟkilē tāṉ iruntavarāka,teṟkiliruntum vaṭakkē ceṉṟu piṉṉum teṟkiṉ kaṇ mīṇṭavarāka, eṅṅaṉam āyiṉum ākuka. avar iṉṉa nāṭṭiṉar, iṉṉa kuṭiyiṉareṉa yām ippoḻutu ārāyntu muṭivu kaṭṭavallē mallēm. avarukkum tamiḻukkum iyaipu kāṭṭit tollōr palarum kūṟiyavai kaḷai oruvāṟu tokutturaittuc ciṟitu ārāycci nikaḻttutalē ippoḻutu ceyaṟpālatu.
